local function GetMagnitude(Part1, Part2)
    return (Part1.Position - Part2.Position).Magnitude
end

local function FWD(parent, part, timeOffset)
    return parent:FindFirstChild(part) or parent:WaitForChild(part, timeOffset or 1)
end

local function CheckNetworkOwnerOnPart(Part) 
    local po = Part:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
    return po and po.Value == plr.Name
end

local function sno(part)
    pcall(function()
        local grabEvents = RS:FindFirstChild("GrabEvents")
        local setNetOwner = grabEvents and grabEvents:FindFirstChild("SetNetworkOwner")
        if setNetOwner then
            setNetOwner:FireServer(part, part.CFrame)
        end
    end)
end

local function CheckForHome()
    local plotItems = workspace:FindFirstChild("PlotItems")
    local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    
    if plots and plotItems then
        for i = 1, 5 do 
            local Plot = plots:FindFirstChild("Plot"..i)
            if Plot then
                local sign = Plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
                local owners = sign and sign:FindFirstChild("ThisPlotsOwners")
                if owners then
                    for _,v in pairs(owners:GetChildren()) do 
                        if v.Value == plr.Name then 
                            return plotItems:FindFirstChild("Plot"..i)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local function SpawnToy(ToyName)
    local InPlot = plr:FindFirstChild("InPlot")
    local InOwnedPlot = plr:FindFirstChild("InOwnedPlot")
    local CanSpawnToy = plr:FindFirstChild("CanSpawnToy")
    local inv = workspace:FindFirstChild(plr.Name.."SpawnedInToys")

    if InPlot and InPlot.Value and InOwnedPlot and not InOwnedPlot.Value then 
        InPlot:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"):Wait()
    end 
    if CanSpawnToy and not CanSpawnToy.Value then 
        CanSpawnToy:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"):Wait()
    end

    local hrp = plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return nil end

    local SpawnCF = (MyPCLD or hrp).CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 14, 20)
    local Container = (InOwnedPlot and InOwnedPlot.Value) and CheckForHome() or inv
    if not Container then return nil end

    local spawnedObject = nil
    local connection
    connection = Container.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        if child.Name == ToyName then
            spawnedObject = child
        end
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        pcall(function()
            local menuToys = RS:FindFirstChild("MenuToys")
            local spawnRemote = menuToys and menuToys:FindFirstChild("SpawnToyRemoteFunction")
            if spawnRemote then
                spawnRemote:InvokeServer(ToyName, SpawnCF, Vector3.zero)
            end
        end)
    end)

    local start = tick()
    repeat task.wait() until spawnedObject or (tick() - start) > 2.5

    if connection then connection:Disconnect() end
    return spawnedObject
end

local function FindPCLD(hrp)
    if pcldConn then pcldConn:Disconnect() end
    MyPCLD = nil
    pcldConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if MyPCLD or not hrp or not hrp.Parent then 
            if pcldConn then pcldConn:Disconnect() pcldConn = nil end
            return
        end
        for _, v in pairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do 
            if v.Name == "PlayerCharacterLocationDetector" and v:IsA("BasePart") then
                if GetMagnitude(v, hrp) <= 2 then 
                    MyPCLD = v
                    break
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

local function fixCharacter(char)
    if not char then return end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if hum and hrp then
        hum.AutoRotate = true
        hum.Sit = false
        hrp.Velocity = Vector3.zero
        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        hrp.CanCollide = true
        hrp.CanTouch = true
        hrp.CanQuery = true
        for _, part in pairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                part.CanCollide = true
                part.CanTouch = true
                part.CanQuery = true
                part.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                part.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end
        end
        hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
    end
end

antiTab:AddToggle({
    Name = "Anti Kick [ITEM]",
    Default = false,
    Flag = "ydc7wwfc",
    Callback = function(Val)
        AntiKickItemActive = Val 
        
        if Val then
            fixCharacter(plr.Character)
            
            if charFixConnection then charFixConnection:Disconnect() end
            charFixConnection = plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
                task.wait(0.5)
                fixCharacter(char)
            end)
            
            task.spawn(function()
                local Item, SoundPart
                while AntiKickItemActive and task.wait() do 
                    local char = plr.Character
                    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                    local inPlot = plr:FindFirstChild("InPlot")
                    local inv = workspace:FindFirstChild(plr.Name.."SpawnedInToys")
                    local destroyToy = RS:FindFirstChild("MenuToys") and RS.MenuToys:FindFirstChild("DestroyToy")
                    
                    if not hrp or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 or not inv then continue end  
                    if inPlot and inPlot.Value then continue end 
                    
                    if not MyPCLD and not pcldConn then
                        FindPCLD(hrp)
                    end

                    Item = inv:FindFirstChild("AntiKickItem") 
                    SoundPart = Item and Item:FindFirstChild("Hitbox")
                    
                    if not Item or not SoundPart then
                        for _,v in pairs(inv:GetChildren()) do 
                            if v.Name == "AntiKickItem" then 
                                pcall(function() destroyToy:FireServer(v) end)
                            end
                        end
                        
                        Item = SpawnToy(SelectedToy)
                        if not Item then continue end 
                        
                        SoundPart = Item and FWD(Item, "Hitbox", 0.5)
                        if SoundPart then sno(SoundPart) end
                        
                        for _,v in pairs(Item:GetChildren()) do 
                            if v:IsA("BasePart") then 
                                v.CanCollide = false 
                                v.Transparency = 0.8
                                v.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 255)
                            end
                        end
                        
                        Item.Name = "AntiKickItem"
                    end
                    
                    if SoundPart and not CheckNetworkOwnerOnPart(SoundPart) then 
                        sno(SoundPart)
                    end
                    
                    local targetPart = MyPCLD or hrp:FindFirstChild("FirePlayerPart") or hrp
                    
                    if SoundPart and targetPart then
                        SoundPart.CFrame = targetPart.CFrame
                        SoundPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        SoundPart.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    end
                end
            end)
        else
            if pcldConn then pcldConn:Disconnect() pcldConn = nil end
            if charFixConnection then
                charFixConnection:Disconnect()
                charFixConnection = nil
            end
            MyPCLD = nil
            
            fixCharacter(plr.Character)
            
            task.spawn(function()
                local inv = workspace:FindFirstChild(plr.Name.."SpawnedInToys")
                local destroyToy = RS:FindFirstChild("MenuToys") and RS.MenuToys:FindFirstChild("DestroyToy")
                if inv and destroyToy then
                    for _,v in pairs(inv:GetChildren()) do 
                        if v.Name == "AntiKickItem" then 
                            pcall(function() destroyToy:FireServer(v) end)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end    
})

plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    if AntiKickItemActive then
        MyPCLD = nil
        local hrp = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
        if hrp then FindPCLD(hrp) end
        task.wait(0.5)
        fixCharacter(char)
    end
end)

end



playerTab:AddSection({
    Name = "Player"
})

local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local run = game:GetService("RunService")

local walkSpeedT = false
local walkSpeedV = 80
local activeLoop = nil
local speedConn = nil

local function updateWalkSpeedF()
    local char = plr.Character
    if not char then return end

    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hum or not root then return end

    hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, false)
    hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, false)
    --hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Tripping, false)

    local speed = (type(walkSpeedV) == "number") and walkSpeedV or 80

    if activeLoop then
        activeLoop:Disconnect()
        activeLoop = nil
    end
    if speedConn then
        speedConn:Disconnect()
        speedConn = nil
    end

    if walkSpeedT then
        hum.WalkSpeed = 16

        speedConn = hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("WalkSpeed"):Connect(function()
            if walkSpeedT and hum.WalkSpeed ~= 16 then
                hum.WalkSpeed = 16
            end
        end)

        activeLoop = run.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not char:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
                if activeLoop then activeLoop:Disconnect() end
                return
            end

            if walkSpeedT then
                local dir = hum.MoveDirection
                if dir.Magnitude == 0 then
                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, 0)
                else
                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(dir.X * speed, root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, dir.Z * speed)
                end

                for _, track in ipairs(hum:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                    local name = track.Name:lower()
                    local id = track.Animation and track.Animation.AnimationId:lower() or ""
                    if name:find("walk") or name:find("run") or id:find("walk") or id:find("run") then
                        track:AdjustSpeed(1)
                    end
                end
            else
                if activeLoop then activeLoop:Disconnect() end
            end
        end)
    else
        hum.WalkSpeed = 16
        hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, true)
        --hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, true)
        --hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Tripping, true)
    end
end

playerTab:AddToggle({
    Name = "Walk speed",
    Default = false,
    Flag = "u9r1dr68hv",
    Callback = function(v)
        walkSpeedT = v
        updateWalkSpeedF()
    end
})

playerTab:AddSlider({
    Name = "Walk speed ",
    Min = 16,
    Max = 500,
    Default = 80,
    Flag = "furiqffuow",
    Increment = 10,
    ValueName = "Speed",
    Callback = function(v)
        walkSpeedV = v
        if walkSpeedT then
            updateWalkSpeedF()
        end
    end
})

plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    char:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
    char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    task.wait(0.1)
    updateWalkSpeedF()
end)

local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local jumpPowerT = false
local jumpPowerV = 50

local function updateJumpPowerF()
    local function apply(char)
        local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid")

        if jumpPowerT then
            hum.UseJumpPower = true
            hum.JumpPower = jumpPowerV
            hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("JumpPower"):Connect(function()
                if jumpPowerT then
                    hum.JumpPower = jumpPowerV
                end
            end)
        else
            hum.UseJumpPower = true
            hum.JumpPower = 50
        end
    end

    if plr.Character then
        apply(plr.Character)
    end

    plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(apply)
end

playerTab:AddSection({
    Name = "Self kill"
})

local Player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local LoopKill = false

local function KillSelf()
    local char = Player.Character
    if char then
        
        for _, v in pairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if v:IsA("JointInstance") or v:IsA("Weld") or v:IsA("Motor6D") then
                v:Destroy()
            end
        end

        
        for _, part in pairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                part.Parent = workspace
                part:BreakJoints()
            end
        end

        
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then
            hum.Health = 0
        end
    end
end

task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(0.1) do
        if LoopKill then
            KillSelf()
        end
    end
end)


playerTab:AddButton({
    Name = "Self kill",
    Callback = function()
        KillSelf()
    end    
})

playerTab:AddToggle({
    Name = "Self loop kill",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        LoopKill = Value
    end    
})
end

    Name = "Other"

    end

local function setWaterWalk(state)
    local workspaceMap = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
    if workspaceMap then
        local alwaysHere = workspaceMap:FindFirstChild("AlwaysHereTweenedObjects")
        if alwaysHere then
            local ocean = alwaysHere:FindFirstChild("Ocean")
            if ocean then
                local object = ocean:FindFirstChild("Object")
                if object then
                    local objectModel = object:FindFirstChild("ObjectModel")
                    if objectModel then
                        for _, child in pairs(objectModel:GetChildren()) do
                            if child:IsA("BasePart") then
                                child.CanCollide = state
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

playerTab:AddToggle({
    Name = "Ocean Walk",
    Default = false,
    Flag = "wipspyhi",
    Callback = function(Value)
        setWaterWalk(Value)
    end    
})
end

    Name = "Infinite Jump",

    save = true,
    Flag = "sosowp0q",
    Callback = function(v)
        _G.InfiniteJump = v
    end

        game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"):ChangeState("Jumping")

do
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer


local function getRoot(char)
    if not char then return nil end
    return char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso") or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
end


local IsOnMobile = UserInputService.TouchEnabled and not UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled


local FLYING = false
local QEfly = true
local iyflyspeed = 1
local vehicleflyspeed = 1
local flyKeyDown, flyKeyUp
local mfly1, mfly2

local velocityHandlerName = "Orion_VelocityHandler"
local gyroHandlerName = "Orion_GyroHandler"


local function sFLY(vfly)
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
    local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not humanoid then
        repeat task.wait() until char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    end

    if flyKeyDown or flyKeyUp then
        flyKeyDown:Disconnect()
        flyKeyUp:Disconnect()
    end

    local T = getRoot(char)
    if not T then return end

    local CONTROL = {F = 0, B = 0, L = 0, R = 0, Q = 0, E = 0}
    local lCONTROL = {F = 0, B = 0, L = 0, R = 0, Q = 0, E = 0}
    local SPEED = 0

    local function FLY()
        FLYING = true
        local BG = Instance.new('BodyGyro')
        local BV = Instance.new('BodyVelocity')
        BG.P = 9e4
        BG.Parent = T
        BV.Parent = T
        BG.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
        BG.CFrame = T.CFrame
        BV.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        BV.MaxForce = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
        
        task.spawn(function()
            repeat task.wait()
                local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
                if not vfly and humanoid then
                    humanoid.PlatformStand = true
                end

                if CONTROL.L + CONTROL.R ~= 0 or CONTROL.F + CONTROL.B ~= 0 or CONTROL.Q + CONTROL.E ~= 0 then
                    SPEED = 50
                elseif not (CONTROL.L + CONTROL.R ~= 0 or CONTROL.F + CONTROL.B ~= 0 or CONTROL.Q + CONTROL.E ~= 0) and SPEED ~= 0 then
                    SPEED = 0
                end
                
                if (CONTROL.L + CONTROL.R) ~= 0 or (CONTROL.F + CONTROL.B) ~= 0 or (CONTROL.Q + CONTROL.E) ~= 0 then
                    BV.Velocity = ((camera.CFrame.LookVector * (CONTROL.F + CONTROL.B)) + ((camera.CFrame * CFrame.new(CONTROL.L + CONTROL.R, (CONTROL.F + CONTROL.B + CONTROL.Q + CONTROL.E) * 0.2, 0).p) - camera.CFrame.p)) * SPEED
                    lCONTROL = {F = CONTROL.F, B = CONTROL.B, L = CONTROL.L, R = CONTROL.R}
                elseif (CONTROL.L + CONTROL.R) == 0 and (CONTROL.F + CONTROL.B) == 0 and (CONTROL.Q + CONTROL.E) == 0 and SPEED ~= 0 then
                    BV.Velocity = ((camera.CFrame.LookVector * (lCONTROL.F + lCONTROL.B)) + ((camera.CFrame * CFrame.new(lCONTROL.L + lCONTROL.R, (lCONTROL.F + lCONTROL.B + CONTROL.Q + CONTROL.E) * 0.2, 0).p) - camera.CFrame.p)) * SPEED
                else
                    BV.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                end
                BG.CFrame = camera.CFrame
            until not FLYING
            
            CONTROL = {F = 0, B = 0, L = 0, R = 0, Q = 0, E = 0}
            lCONTROL = {F = 0, B = 0, L = 0, R = 0, Q = 0, E = 0}
            SPEED = 0
            
            if BG then BG:Destroy() end
            if BV then BV:Destroy() end
            if humanoid then humanoid.PlatformStand = false end
        end)
    end

    flyKeyDown = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
        if processed then return end
        if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.W then
            CONTROL.F = (vfly and vehicleflyspeed or iyflyspeed)
        elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.S then
            CONTROL.B = - (vfly and vehicleflyspeed or iyflyspeed)
        elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.A then
            CONTROL.L = - (vfly and vehicleflyspeed or iyflyspeed)
        elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.D then
            CONTROL.R = (vfly and vehicleflyspeed or iyflyspeed)
        elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.E and QEfly then
            CONTROL.Q = (vfly and vehicleflyspeed or iyflyspeed)*2
        elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Q and QEfly then
            CONTROL.E = -(vfly and vehicleflyspeed or iyflyspeed)*2
        end
        pcall(function() workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Track end)
    end)

    flyKeyUp = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input, processed)
        if processed then return end
        if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.W then CONTROL.F = 0
        elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.S then CONTROL.B = 0
        elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.A then CONTROL.L = 0
        elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.D then CONTROL.R = 0
        elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.E then CONTROL.Q = 0
        elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Q then CONTROL.E = 0
        end
    end)
    FLY()
end

local function NOFLY()
    FLYING = false
    if flyKeyDown then flyKeyDown:Disconnect() end
    if flyKeyUp then flyKeyUp:Disconnect() end
    if LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass('Humanoid') then
        LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass('Humanoid').PlatformStand = false
    end
    pcall(function() workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom end)
end


function unmobilefly(speaker)
    pcall(function()
        FLYING = false
        local root = getRoot(speaker.Character)
        if root then
            if root:FindFirstChild(velocityHandlerName) then root:FindFirstChild(velocityHandlerName):Destroy() end
            if root:FindFirstChild(gyroHandlerName) then root:FindFirstChild(gyroHandlerName):Destroy() end
        end
        if speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid") then
            speaker.Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid").PlatformStand = false
        end
        if mfly1 then mfly1:Disconnect() end
        if mfly2 then mfly2:Disconnect() end
    end)
end

local function mobilefly(speaker, vfly)
    unmobilefly(speaker)
    FLYING = true

    local root = getRoot(speaker.Character)
    if not root then return end

    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local v3none = Vector3.new()
    local v3zero = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
    local v3inf = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)

    
    local controlModule = require(speaker.PlayerScripts:WaitForChild("PlayerModule"):WaitForChild("ControlModule"))
    
    local bv = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
    bv.Name = velocityHandlerName
    bv.Parent = root
    bv.MaxForce = v3zero
    bv.Velocity = v3zero

    local bg = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
    bg.Name = gyroHandlerName
    bg.Parent = root
    bg.MaxTorque = v3inf
    bg.P = 1000
    bg.D = 50

    mfly1 = speaker.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
        local newRoot = getRoot(newChar)
        if not newRoot then return end
        
        local nbv = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
        nbv.Name = velocityHandlerName
        nbv.Parent = newRoot
        nbv.MaxForce = v3zero
        nbv.Velocity = v3zero

        local nbg = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
        nbg.Name = gyroHandlerName
        nbg.Parent = newRoot
        nbg.MaxTorque = v3inf
        nbg.P = 1000
        nbg.D = 50
    end)

    mfly2 = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        root = getRoot(speaker.Character)
        camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        
        if speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid") and root and root:FindFirstChild(velocityHandlerName) and root:FindFirstChild(gyroHandlerName) then
            local humanoid = speaker.Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")
            local VelocityHandler = root:FindFirstChild(velocityHandlerName)
            local GyroHandler = root:FindFirstChild(gyroHandlerName)

            VelocityHandler.MaxForce = v3inf
            GyroHandler.MaxTorque = v3inf
            if not vfly then humanoid.PlatformStand = true end
            GyroHandler.CFrame = camera.CoordinateFrame
            VelocityHandler.Velocity = v3none

            local direction = controlModule:GetMoveVector()
            
            if direction.X > 0 or direction.X < 0 then
                VelocityHandler.Velocity = VelocityHandler.Velocity + camera.CFrame.RightVector * (direction.X * ((vfly and vehicleflyspeed or iyflyspeed) * 50))
            end
            if direction.Z > 0 or direction.Z < 0 then
                VelocityHandler.Velocity = VelocityHandler.Velocity - camera.CFrame.LookVector * (direction.Z * ((vfly and vehicleflyspeed or iyflyspeed) * 50))
            end
        end
    end)
end



playerTab:AddToggle({
    Name = "Fly (fix)",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        if Value then
            
            if not IsOnMobile then
                NOFLY()
                task.wait()
                sFLY()
            else
                mobilefly(LocalPlayer)
            end
        else
            
            if not IsOnMobile then
                NOFLY()
            else
                unmobilefly(LocalPlayer)
            end
        end
    end    
})

AuraTab:AddToggle({
    Name = "Anti kick Anti aura",
    Default = false,
      Flag = "o71rciy",
    Callback = function(Value)
        removeAntiKickActive = Value
        
        if removeAntiKickActive then
            task.spawn(function()
                local RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
                local Players = game:GetService("Players")
                local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
                local GrabEvents = RS:WaitForChild("GrabEvents")
                local SetNetOwner = GrabEvents:WaitForChild("SetNetworkOwner")
                
                while removeAntiKickActive do
                    local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
                    local myRoot = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if not myRoot then 
                        task.wait(0.1)
                        continue 
                    end
                    
                    for _, target in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                        if target ~= LocalPlayer then
                            if useWhitelistRemoveAntiKick and LocalPlayer:IsFriendsWith(target.UserId) then
                                continue
                            end
                            
                            local tChar = target.Character
                            local tRoot = tChar and tChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                            if not tRoot then continue end
                            
                           if (tRoot.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude <= removeAntiKickRadius then
                                local spawned = workspace:FindFirstChild(target.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
                                if spawned then
                                    for _, toyName in ipairs({"NinjaKunai", "NinjaShuriken", "AntiKick"}) do
                                        local toy = spawned:FindFirstChild(toyName)
                                        if toy then
                                            local part = toy:FindFirstChild("SoundPart")
                                            if part then
                                                pcall(function()
                                                    SetNetOwner:FireServer(part, part.CFrame)
                                                end)
                                                if part:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") and part.PartOwner.Value == LocalPlayer.Name then
                                                    part.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 1000000, 0)
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    task.wait(0.1)
                end
            end)
        end
    end    
})

AuraTab:AddToggle({
    Name = "Ragdoll Aura <font color='rgb(255, 50, 50)'>[Ownership network]</font>",
    Default = false,
    save = true,
    Flag = "NetworkOwnerAura",
    Callback = function(enabled)
        runningNetOwnerAura = enabled
        if enabled then
            task.spawn(function()
                local Players = game:GetService("Players")
                local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
                local rs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
                local SetNetworkOwner = rs:FindFirstChild("GrabEvents") and rs.GrabEvents:FindFirstChild("SetNetworkOwner")
                
                while runningNetOwnerAura do
                    pcall(function()
                        local character = localPlayer.Character
                        local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if root then
                            local currentRadius = tonumber(auraRadius) or 30
                            
                            for _, player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                                if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character then
                                    local isFriend = _G.Aurahowait and localPlayer:IsFriendsWith(player.UserId)
                                    if not isFriend then
                                        local targetChar = player.Character
                                        local torso = targetChar:FindFirstChild("Torso") or targetChar:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
                                        local targetRoot = targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                        
                                        if torso and targetRoot then
                                            local distance = (torso.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                                            if distance <= currentRadius then
                                                if SetNetworkOwner and SetNetworkOwner.FireServer then
                                                    SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(torso, targetRoot.CFrame)
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                    task.wait()
                end
            end)
        end
    end
})

plotTab:AddButton({
    Name = "TP",
    Callback = function()
local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local char = plr.Character or plr.CharacterAdded:Wait()
local hrp = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")

local pos = teleportLocations[selectedLocation]  
    if not pos then  
        return  
    end  

    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos[1], pos[2], pos[3])  
end,

})
       -- local Players = game:GetService("Players")
--local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local HRP
local enabled = false
local returnPos

function waitExact(t)
    local s = os.clock()
    repeat task.wait() until os.clock() - s >= t
end

local function setChar(c)
    HRP = c:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart",5)
end

if player.Character then setChar(player.Character) end
player.CharacterAdded:Connect(setChar)

local function getSlot()
    local f = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Slots")
    if not f then return end
    local t = f:GetChildren()
    if #t == 0 then return end
    return t[math.random(1,#t)]
end

 function getTime(slot)
    return slot:FindFirstChild("TimeText",true)
end

local function loop(label)
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            if not enabled then waitExact(1) continue end

            local s = getSlot()
            local h = s and s:FindFirstChild("Handle",true)
            local t = s and getTime(s)

            if h and t and HRP then
                while t.Text ~= "0:00" and enabled do
                    label:Set("Time: "..t.Text)
                    waitExact(0.3)
                end

                if not enabled then continue end

                returnPos = HRP.CFrame

                HRP.CFrame = CFrame.new(h.Position + Vector3.new(0,-3,0))
                waitExact(0.5)
                HRP.Anchored = true
                waitExact(0.5)

                ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("GrabEvents"):WaitForChild("SetNetworkOwner"):FireServer(h,CFrame.new(h.Position))

                waitExact(0.8)
                HRP.Anchored = false

                waitExact(1)

                if returnPos then
                    HRP.CFrame = returnPos
                    returnPos = nil
                end
            else
                label:Set("Time: ...")
                waitExact(0.5)
            end
        end
    end)
end
do
plotTab:AddSection({
    Name = "get plot"
})

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local repStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

local plotCFrames = {
    [1] = CFrame.new(-532.0158081054688, -5.591518878936768, 95.6521987915039, -0.865190327167511, -0.2596333920955658, 0.42899444699287415, 0, 0.8555188179016113, 0.51777184009552, -0.50144362449646, 0.4479711949825287, -0.7401865720748901),
    [2] = CFrame.new(-493.17340087890625, -5.653066158294678, -164.51084899902344, 0.30851930379867554, -0.37542006373405457, 0.8739997744560242, 0, 0.9188216924667358, 0.3946729600429535, -0.9512181282043457, -0.12176422774791718, 0.2834742069244385),
    [3] = CFrame.new(256.76751708984375, -5.380659103393555, 466.65625, -0.8961027264595032, 0.30602845549583435, -0.321475625038147, 0, 0.7242941856384277, 0.6894912123680115, 0.44384679198265076, 0.6178549528121948, -0.6490419507026672),
    [4] = CFrame.new(513.7680053710938, 85.13038635253906, -345.8212585449219, 0.4605468213558197, 0.5212469696998596, -0.7184693813323975, 0, 0.8094194531440735, 0.5872309803962708, 0.887635350227356, -0.27044737339019775, 0.3727755844593048),
    [5] = CFrame.new(559.8180541992188, 125.14988708496094, -76.7403335571289, 0.02620585821568966, 0.622352659702301, -0.7822982668876648, 0, 0.7825670838356018, 0.6225664615631104, 0.999656617641449, -0.01631488837301731, 0.02050784043967724)
}

local selectedPlotNum = 1

plotTab:AddDropdown({
    Name = "Select House",
    Default = "Green House",
    Options = {"Green House", "Pink House", "Witch House (Purple)", "Blue House", "Red House"},
    Callback = function(Value)
        local num = string.match(Value, "%d+")
        if num then
            selectedPlotNum = tonumber(num)
        end
    end
})


plotTab:AddButton({
    Name = "get plot",
    Callback = function()
        local character = player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
        local rootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local setNetworkOwner = repStorage:FindFirstChild("GrabEvents") and repStorage.GrabEvents:FindFirstChild("SetNetworkOwner")
        
        if not rootPart or not setNetworkOwner then return end

        local ownedPlot = nil
        for i = 1, 5 do
            local plot = workspace.Plots:FindFirstChild("Plot" .. i)
            if plot then
                local ownersFolder = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign") and plot.PlotSign:FindFirstChild("ThisPlotsOwners")
                if ownersFolder then
                    for _, child in pairs(ownersFolder:GetChildren()) do
                        local success, val = pcall(function() return tostring(child.Value) end)
                        if (success and string.find(val, player.Name)) or string.find(child.Name, player.Name) then
                            ownedPlot = i
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            if ownedPlot then break end
        end

        if ownedPlot then
            if OrionLib then
                OrionLib:MakeNotification({
                    Name = "[  I've already acquired the house. ]",
                    Content = " Plot" .. ownedPlot .. "",
                    Time = 4
                })
            end
            return
        end

        local targetPlotNum = selectedPlotNum
        local originalPosition = rootPart.CFrame
        local targetCFrame = plotCFrames[targetPlotNum]
        
        local plotFolder = workspace.Plots:FindFirstChild("Plot" .. targetPlotNum)
        local grabPart = plotFolder and plotFolder:FindFirstChild("PlotSign") and plotFolder.PlotSign:FindFirstChild("Sign") and plotFolder.PlotSign.Sign:FindFirstChild("Plus") and plotFolder.PlotSign.Sign.Plus:FindFirstChild("PlusGrabPart")

        if not grabPart then return end

        local maxAttempts = 3
        local acquired = false

        for attempt = 1, maxAttempts do
            rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            rootPart.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            rootPart.CFrame = targetCFrame

            task.wait(0.07)

            local timeElapsed = 0
            local duration = 0.3

            while timeElapsed < duration do
                pcall(function()
                    setNetworkOwner:FireServer(grabPart, targetCFrame)
                end)
                timeElapsed = timeElapsed + task.wait(0.03)
            end

            rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            rootPart.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            rootPart.CFrame = originalPosition

            local checkTimeout = 0.5
            local checkTimeElapsed = 0

            while checkTimeElapsed < checkTimeout do
                local plotToCheck = workspace.Plots:FindFirstChild("Plot" .. targetPlotNum)
                if plotToCheck then
                    local ownersFolder = plotToCheck:FindFirstChild("PlotSign") and plotToCheck.PlotSign:FindFirstChild("ThisPlotsOwners")
                    if ownersFolder then
                        for _, child in pairs(ownersFolder:GetChildren()) do
                            local success, val = pcall(function() return tostring(child.Value) end)
                            if (success and string.find(val, player.Name)) or string.find(child.Name, player.Name) then
                                acquired = true
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end

                if acquired then break end
                checkTimeElapsed = checkTimeElapsed + task.wait(0.03)
            end

            if acquired then break end
        end

        if OrionLib then
            if acquired then
                OrionLib:MakeNotification({
                    Name = "[ get plot ]",
                    Content = "Plot" .. targetPlotNum .. " ",
                    Time = 4
                })
            else
                OrionLib:MakeNotification({
                    Name = "[ get plot ]",
                    Content = "fail",
                    Time = 4
                })
            end
        end
    end
})
end

    Name = "auto slot"
})

plotTab:AddToggle({
    Name="Auto slot",
    Default=false,
    Flag = "uot1fugwo",
    Callback=function(v)
        enabled = v
        if v then loop(label) end
    end
})

funTab:AddButton({
	Name = "Making",
	Callback = function()
		local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
		local TNUM = tonumber(InputValue)

		if not TNUM or TNUM <= 0 then
			warn("Please enter a valid number!")
			OrionLib:MakeNotification({
				Name = "[dick ]",
				Content = "Write down the numbers.",
				Time = 3
			})
			return
		end

		local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
		local character = plr.Character or plr.CharacterAdded:Wait()
		local hrp = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		local StickyPartEvent = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("PlayerEvents"):WaitForChild("StickyPartEvent")
		local SpawnToy = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("MenuToys"):WaitForChild("SpawnToyRemoteFunction")

		function GetPlotNumber()
			local char = plr.Character
			if not char then return nil end

			if char.Parent == workspace then 
				return nil
			elseif char.Parent.Name == "PlayersInPlots" then
				for _, plot in workspace.Plots:GetChildren() do
					for _, owner in plot.PlotSign.ThisPlotsOwners:GetChildren() do
						if owner.Value == plr.Name then
							return tonumber(plot.Name:match("%d+"))
						end
					end
				end
			end
			return nil
		end

		function GetInventory()
			local plotNumber = GetPlotNumber()

			if plotNumber then
				local plotItems = workspace:FindFirstChild("PlotItems")
				if plotItems then
					local plotFolder = plotItems:FindFirstChild("Plot" .. plotNumber)
					if plotFolder then
						return plotFolder
					end
				end
			end
			return workspace:WaitForChild(plr.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
		end

		local function createPencil(index)
			while not plr.CanSpawnToy.Value do task.wait() end

			task.spawn(function()
				SpawnToy:InvokeServer("ToolPencil", hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0,5,15), Vector3.new(0,0,0))
			end)

			task.wait(0.3)

			local inv = GetInventory()

			local pencil
			for i = #inv:GetChildren(), 1, -1 do
				local obj = inv:GetChildren()[i]
				if obj.Name == "ToolPencil" then
					pencil = obj
					break
				end
			end

			if not pencil then return nil end

			local stickyPart = pencil:WaitForChild("StickyPart")
			local SoundPart = pencil:WaitForChild("SoundPart")

			if stickyPart and SoundPart then
				ReplicatedStorage.GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(SoundPart, SoundPart.CFrame)
				ReplicatedStorage.GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(SoundPart, character:FindFirstChild("Torso") and character.Torso.CFrame or hrp.CFrame)
			end

			local PartOwner = SoundPart:WaitForChild("PartOwner")
			if PartOwner then
				SoundPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 599999, 999)
			end

			stickyPart.Name = "w" .. index

			for _, part in pairs(pencil:GetDescendants()) do
				if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Color == Color3.fromRGB(158, 108, 141) then
					part.Name = "a" .. index
				end
			end

			return pencil
		end

		local pencils = {}

		for i = 1, TNUM do
			local pencil = createPencil(i)
			if pencil then
				table.insert(pencils, pencil)
			end
			task.wait(0.1)
		end

		for i = 1, #pencils - 1 do
			local current = pencils[i]
			local nextP = pencils[i + 1]

			if current and nextP then
				local from = current:FindFirstChild("w" .. i)
				local to = nextP:FindFirstChild("a" .. (i + 1))

				if from and to then
					StickyPartEvent:FireServer(from, to, CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(-90), 0))
				end
			end
			task.wait(0.05)
		end

		local torso2 = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart"):WaitForChild("RagdollTouchedHitbox")

		if torso2 and #pencils >= TNUM then
			local last = pencils[TNUM]
			if last then
				local wLast = last:FindFirstChild("w" .. TNUM)
				if wLast then
					local attachCF = torso2.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -1.1, 0.1) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(180), 0)
					local relativeCF = torso2.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(attachCF)
					StickyPartEvent:FireServer(wLast, torso2, relativeCF)
				end
			end
		end
	end
})



funTab:AddSection({
	Name = "various things"
})

	Name = "Kunai",
	Default = "",
	TextDisappear = false,
	Callback = function(Value)
		InputValue = Value
	end
})





funTab:AddButton({
	Name = "neutralization",
	Callback = function()
		local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
		local Players = game:GetService("Players")
		local plr = Players.LocalPlayer
		
		local TNUM = tonumber(InputValue)

		if not TNUM or TNUM <= 0 or TNUM > 100 then
			OrionLib:MakeNotification({
				Name = "[ ? ]",
				Content = "Invalid Value",
				Time = 3
			})
			return
		end

		local character = plr.Character or plr.CharacterAdded:Wait()
		local hrp = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local torso = character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
		
		if not torso then return end
		
		local StickyPartEvent = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("PlayerEvents"):WaitForChild("StickyPartEvent")
		local SpawnToy = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("MenuToys"):WaitForChild("SpawnToyRemoteFunction")

		local function GetTargetPartFromGrabParts()
			local grabPartsModel = workspace:FindFirstChild("GrabParts")
			if not grabPartsModel then return nil end

			local grabPart = grabPartsModel:FindFirstChild("GrabPart")
			if not grabPart then return nil end

			local weldConstraint = grabPart:FindFirstChild("WeldConstraint")
			if not weldConstraint or not weldConstraint:IsA("WeldConstraint") then return nil end

			return weldConstraint.Part1
		end

		local function GetPlotNumber()
			local char = plr.Character
			if not char then return nil end

			if char.Parent == workspace then 
				return nil
			elseif char.Parent.Name == "PlayersInPlots" then
				for _, plot in workspace.Plots:GetChildren() do
					local plotSign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
					if plotSign and plotSign:FindFirstChild("ThisPlotsOwners") then
						for _, owner in plotSign.ThisPlotsOwners:GetChildren() do
							if owner.Value == plr.Name then
								return tonumber(plot.Name:match("%d+"))
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
			return nil
		end

		local function GetInventory()
			local plotNumber = GetPlotNumber()

			if plotNumber then
				local plotItems = workspace:FindFirstChild("PlotItems")
				if plotItems then
					local plotFolder = plotItems:FindFirstChild("Plot" .. plotNumber)
					if plotFolder then
						return plotFolder
					end
				end
			end
			
			return workspace:FindFirstChild(plr.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
		end

		local targetPart = GetTargetPartFromGrabParts()
		if not targetPart then return end

		local function createAndAttachKunai()
			while not plr.CanSpawnToy.Value do task.wait(0.1) end

			task.spawn(function()
				SpawnToy:InvokeServer("NinjaKunai", hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0,5,15), Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
			end)

			task.wait(0.2)

			local inv = GetInventory()
			if not inv then return nil end

			local kunai
			for i = #inv:GetChildren(), 1, -1 do
				local obj = inv:GetChildren()[i]
				if obj.Name == "NinjaKunai" then
					kunai = obj
					break
				end
			end

			if not kunai then return nil end

			local stickyPart = kunai:WaitForChild("StickyPart")
			local SoundPart = kunai:WaitForChild("SoundPart")

			if stickyPart and SoundPart then
				pcall(function()
					ReplicatedStorage.GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(SoundPart, SoundPart.CFrame)
					ReplicatedStorage.GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(SoundPart, torso.CFrame)
				end)
			end

			if stickyPart then
				local relativeCF = targetPart.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(targetPart.CFrame)
				pcall(function()
					StickyPartEvent:FireServer(stickyPart, targetPart, relativeCF)
				end)
			end

			task.wait(0.1)
			return kunai
		end

		for i = 1, TNUM do
			createAndAttachKunai()
			task.wait(0.1)
		end
	end
})

local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local coreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")

local KeyConnection = nil

local function RemoveGui()
    if coreGui:FindFirstChild("NoClipGui") then
        coreGui.NoClipGui:Destroy()
    end
end

local function DisconnectKey()
    if KeyConnection then
        KeyConnection:Disconnect()
        KeyConnection = nil
    end
end

local function NoClipPart()
    if Processing then return end
    Processing = true

    local char = plr.Character
    if not char then
        Processing = false
        return
    end

    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local torso = char:FindFirstChild("Torso") or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
    if not hrp or not torso then
        Processing = false
        return
    end

    local tt = mouse.Target
    if not tt or not tt:IsA("BasePart") then 
        Processing = false
        return
    end

    if tt.Name == "PlotBarrier" then 
        local success, check = pcall(function()
            return tt.Parent and tt.Parent.Parent and tt.Parent.Parent:FindFirstChild("PlotArea")
        end)
        if success and check and check:IsA("BasePart") then
            tt = check
        else
            Processing = false
            return
        end
    end

    if tt.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" then 
        local success, check = pcall(function()
            return tt.Parent and tt.Parent:FindFirstChild("Torso")
        end)
        if success and check and check:IsA("BasePart") then
            tt = check
        else
            Processing = false
            return
        end
    end

    local inv = workspace:FindFirstChild(plr.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
    if not inv then
        Processing = false
        return
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        pcall(function()
            rs.MenuToys.SpawnToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer("NinjaShuriken", hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 5, 20), Vector3.zero)
        end)
    end)

    local kunai = nil
    for i = 1, 30 do
        task.wait(0.1)
        kunai = inv:FindFirstChild("NinjaShuriken")
        if kunai then break end
    end

    if not kunai then 
        Processing = false
        return 
    end

    local stickyPart = kunai:FindFirstChild("StickyPart")
    local soundPart = kunai:FindFirstChild("SoundPart")

    if stickyPart and soundPart then
        pcall(function()
            rs.GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(soundPart, CFrame.lookAt(torso.Position, soundPart.Position))
            task.wait(0.1)
            rs.PlayerEvents.StickyPartEvent:FireServer(stickyPart, tt, CFrame.new(107.5, 9.00000049e+33, -3.86856262e+25, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0.0662516952, -0.997802973, 0, 0.997802973, 0.0662516952))
        end)
    end

    kunai.Name = ""
    Processing = false
end


funTab:AddToggle({
    Name = "Noclip target(Q)",
    Default = false,
    Flag = "wgutowutog",
    Callback = function(Value)
        if Value then
            RemoveGui()
            DisconnectKey()

            KeyConnection = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
                if gameProcessed then return end
                if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Q then
                    NoClipPart()
                end
            end)

            if UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
                local ScreenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
                ScreenGui.Name = "NoClipGui"
                ScreenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
                ScreenGui.Parent = coreGui

                local ActionButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
                ActionButton.Name = "ActionButton"
                ActionButton.Parent = ScreenGui
                ActionButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 100, 0, 35)
                ActionButton.Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0.5, -17)
                ActionButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 35)
                ActionButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
                ActionButton.Text = "NoClip"
                ActionButton.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                ActionButton.TextSize = 14
                ActionButton.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
                ActionButton.Active = true
                ActionButton.Draggable = true

                local UICorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
                UICorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
                UICorner.Parent = ActionButton

                local UIStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
                UIStroke.Thickness = 1.5
                UIStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 80)
                UIStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
                UIStroke.Parent = ActionButton

                ActionButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    NoClipPart()
                end)
            end
        else
            RemoveGui()
            DisconnectKey()
        end
    end
})

do
	local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
	local Players = game:GetService("Players")
	local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
	local ContextActionService = game:GetService("ContextActionService")
	local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
	local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
	local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
	local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

	local REMOTE_NAME = "ControlEvent"
	local targetRemote = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild(REMOTE_NAME, true)

	_G.ControllingCreature = nil

	local function safeGetCharacter()
		if typeof(GetPlayerCharacter) == "function" then
			local char = GetPlayerCharacter()
			if char then return char end
		end
		return LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
	end

	local function safeTeleport(cframe)
		if typeof(TeleportPlayer) == "function" then
			TeleportPlayer(cframe)
		else
			local char = safeGetCharacter()
			if char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
				char.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = cframe
			end
		end
	end

	local function makeCharacterNotGrabbable(model)
		if typeof(model) == "Instance" and model:IsA("Model") then
			for _, child in ipairs(model:GetChildren()) do
				if child:IsA("BasePart") then
					child.CanQuery = false
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local function makeCharacterGrabbable(model)
		if typeof(model) == "Instance" and model:IsA("Model") then
			for _, child in ipairs(model:GetChildren()) do
				if child:IsA("BasePart") then
					child.CanQuery = true
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local function setCharacterNoclip(character, noclipState)
		if typeof(character) == "Instance" and character:IsA("Model") then
			for _, part in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
				if part:IsA("BasePart") then
					part.CanCollide = not noclipState
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local function sendFireServer(action, model)
		if not targetRemote or not targetRemote.Parent then
			targetRemote = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild(REMOTE_NAME, true)
		end
		if targetRemote and targetRemote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
			pcall(function()
				targetRemote:FireServer(action, model)
			end)
		end
	end

	local controlSoundEffect = Instance.new('Sound', workspace)
	controlSoundEffect.SoundId = 'rbxassetid://9126228625'
	controlSoundEffect.PlaybackSpeed = 1.25

	local function controlCreature(targetModel)
		if typeof(targetModel) == 'Instance' and targetModel:IsA('Model') then
			local targetHumanoid = targetModel:FindFirstChildOfClass('Humanoid')
			local targetRootPart = targetModel:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')

			if targetModel and targetHumanoid and targetRootPart then
				local activeConnections = {}

				local function cleanupConnections()
					for _, connection in pairs(activeConnections) do
						if typeof(connection) == 'RBXScriptConnection' then
							connection:Disconnect()
						end
					end
					table.clear(activeConnections)
				end

				_G.ControllingCreature = targetModel
				targetHumanoid.WalkSpeed = 0
				targetHumanoid.JumpPower = 24

				sendFireServer("StartControl", targetModel)

				activeConnections[1] = targetHumanoid.Died:Connect(function()
					_G.ControllingCreature = nil
				end)

				local targetBodyVelocity = Instance.new('BodyVelocity', targetRootPart)
				local myBodyVelocity = Instance.new('BodyVelocity')

				myBodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(0, math.huge, 0)
				myBodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.new()
				targetBodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, 0, math.huge)

				makeCharacterNotGrabbable(targetModel)
				
				controlSoundEffect:Play()

				local myCharacter = safeGetCharacter()
				local myRootPart, myHumanoid

				if myCharacter then
					myHumanoid = myCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass('Humanoid')
					myRootPart = myCharacter:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
					myBodyVelocity.Parent = myRootPart

					if myHumanoid then
						activeConnections[2] = myHumanoid.Died:Connect(function()
							_G.ControllingCreature = nil
						end)
					end

					activeConnections[3] = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
						sendFireServer("StartControl", targetModel)

						if myHumanoid and targetBodyVelocity and targetHumanoid then
							local moveDir = myHumanoid.MoveDirection
							targetBodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(moveDir.X * 20, targetRootPart.Velocity.Y, moveDir.Z * 20)
							
							if moveDir.Magnitude > 0 then
								local targetCFrame = CFrame.new(targetRootPart.Position, targetRootPart.Position + Vector3.new(moveDir.X, 0, moveDir.Z))
								targetRootPart.CFrame = targetRootPart.CFrame:Lerp(targetCFrame, 0.25)
							end
						end
					end)

					activeConnections[4] = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
						if myCharacter and myCharacter.Parent then
							setCharacterNoclip(myCharacter, true)
						end

						local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
						if cam and targetHumanoid then
							cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
							cam.CameraSubject = targetHumanoid
							targetHumanoid.CameraOffset = Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0)
						end
					end)

					activeConnections[5] = UserInputService.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
						if targetHumanoid then
							targetHumanoid:ChangeState('Jumping')
						end
					end)

					targetHumanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, false)
				end

				while targetModel.Parent and _G.ControllingCreature == targetModel and (myCharacter and myCharacter.Parent) do
					safeTeleport(CFrame.new(targetRootPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, -20, 0)))
					task.wait()
				end

				cleanupConnections()

				sendFireServer("StopControl", targetModel)

				if myCharacter then
					setCharacterNoclip(myCharacter, false)
				end

				safeTeleport(CFrame.new(targetRootPart.Position + Vector3.new(3, 3, 3)))
				makeCharacterGrabbable(targetModel)

				targetBodyVelocity:Destroy()
				myBodyVelocity:Destroy()

				if myHumanoid then
					myHumanoid:Move(Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
					myHumanoid.CameraOffset = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
				end

				if myRootPart then
					myRootPart.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
					myRootPart.RotVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
					if myRootPart:IsA("BasePart") then
						myRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
						myRootPart.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
					end
				end

				local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
				if myHumanoid and cam then
					cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
					cam.CameraSubject = myHumanoid
				end
				
				_G.ControllingCreature = nil
			end
		end
	end

	local function controlBindF()
		local myCharacter = safeGetCharacter()

		if myCharacter then
			local myHead = myCharacter:FindFirstChild("Head")
			local currentCamInst = workspace.CurrentCamera
			local myHumanoid = myCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass('Humanoid')
			
			if myHead and currentCamInst and myHumanoid and myHumanoid.Health > 0 then
				local rayFilter = RaycastParams.new()
				rayFilter.FilterDescendantsInstances = { myCharacter }
				rayFilter.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude

				local raycastResult = workspace:Raycast(myHead.Position, currentCamInst.CFrame.lookVector * 50, rayFilter)

				if raycastResult and raycastResult.Instance then
					local hitModel = raycastResult.Instance.Parent

					while hitModel and hitModel ~= workspace and not hitModel:FindFirstChildOfClass('Humanoid') do
						hitModel = hitModel.Parent
					end

					if hitModel and hitModel:FindFirstChildOfClass('Humanoid') then
						controlCreature(hitModel)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local function controlBind(actionName, inputState, _)
		if actionName == 'Control(C)' and inputState == Enum.UserInputState.Begin then
			if _G.ControllingCreature then
				_G.ControllingCreature = nil
			else
				controlBindF()
			end
		end
	end

	local gui2 = Instance.new('ScreenGui')
	gui2.ResetOnSpawn = false
	gui2.Name = 'CAG2'

	if UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
		gui2.Parent = LocalPlayer.PlayerGui
	end

	local imageButtonControl = Instance.new('ImageButton')
	imageButtonControl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 50, 0, 50)
	imageButtonControl.Position = UDim2.new(1, -378, 1, -80)
	imageButtonControl.Image = 'rbxassetid://97166444'
	imageButtonControl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	imageButtonControl.ImageTransparency = 0.2
	imageButtonControl.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(142, 142, 142)
	imageButtonControl.Parent = gui2

	local imageCLabel = Instance.new('ImageLabel')
	imageCLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	imageCLabel.Image = 'rbxassetid://14436167187'
	imageCLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	imageCLabel.Parent = imageButtonControl

	imageButtonControl.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
		if not processed and UserInputService.TouchEnabled and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			controlBind('Control(C)', Enum.UserInputState.Begin)
		end
	end)

	if typeof(funTab) == "table" and typeof(funTab.AddToggle) == "function" then
		funTab:AddToggle({
			Name = 'Control (C)',
			Default = false,
			Save = true,
			Flag = 'controlbind_toggle',
			Callback = function(enabled)
				imageButtonControl.Visible = enabled
				imageButtonControl.Active = enabled

				if enabled then
					ContextActionService:BindAction('Control(C)', controlBind, false, Enum.KeyCode.C)
				else
					ContextActionService:UnbindAction('Control(C)')
					_G.ControllingCreature = nil
				end
			end
		})
	else
		ContextActionService:BindAction('Control(C)', controlBind, false, Enum.KeyCode.C)
	end
end
do

--local OrionLib = loadstring(game:HttpGet(('https://raw.githubusercontent.com/shlexware/Orion/main/source')))()
--local Window = OrionLib:MakeWindow({Name = "Black Hole Hub", HidePremium = false, SaveConfig = false, ConfigFolder = "BHKickConfig"})

local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local CustomModelId = "rbxassetid://111278758469077"
local CustomModelAsset = nil

pcall(function()
    CustomModelAsset = game:GetObjects(CustomModelId)[1]
end)

local FeatureEnabled = false
local ActiveConnections = {}
local CreatedModels = {}

local function ScaleNumberSequence(seq, scale)
    if not seq then return end
    local keypoints = {}
    for _, kp in ipairs(seq.Keypoints) do
        table.insert(keypoints, NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(kp.Time, kp.Value * scale, kp.Envelope * scale))
    end
    return NumberSequence.new(keypoints)
end

local function ScaleNumberRange(range, scale)
    if not range then return end
    return NumberRange.new(range.Min * scale, range.Max * scale)
end

local function CleanupCustomizations()
    for _, conn in pairs(ActiveConnections) do
        if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
    end
    table.clear(ActiveConnections)

    for _, model in pairs(CreatedModels) do
        if model and model.Parent then
            model:Destroy()
        end
    end
    table.clear(CreatedModels)

    for _, obj in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj.Name == "Hole" and obj:IsA("BasePart") then
            obj.Transparency = 0
            obj:SetAttribute("HasCustomHole", nil)
        elseif obj.Name == "BillboardGui" and obj:IsA("BillboardGui") then
            obj.Enabled = true
        end
    end
end

local function ApplyChangesToObj(d)
    if not FeatureEnabled then return end

    if d.Name == 'Drone' then
        task.defer(function()
            if d and d.Parent then d:Destroy() end
        end)
        return
    end

    if d.Name == 'BillboardGui' and d:IsA('BillboardGui') then
        local function HideGui()
            if not FeatureEnabled then return end
            d.Enabled = false
            for _, f in pairs(d:GetDescendants()) do
                pcall(function() f.BackgroundTransparency = 1 end)
                pcall(function() f.TextTransparency = 1 end)
                pcall(function() f.ImageTransparency = 1 end)
            end
        end
        HideGui()
        table.insert(ActiveConnections, d.Changed:Connect(HideGui))
        table.insert(ActiveConnections, d.DescendantAdded:Connect(HideGui))

    elseif d.Name == 'Hole' and d:IsA('BasePart') then
        task.wait(0.1)
        if not FeatureEnabled then return end
        d.Transparency = 1
        
        table.insert(ActiveConnections, d:GetPropertyChangedSignal('Transparency'):Connect(function()
            if FeatureEnabled and d.Transparency ~= 1 then 
                d.Transparency = 1 
            end
        end))

        if CustomModelAsset and not d:GetAttribute('HasCustomHole') then
            d:SetAttribute('HasCustomHole', true)
            local customModel = CustomModelAsset:Clone()
            customModel.Name = 'CustomHoleModel'
            customModel.Parent = workspace
            table.insert(CreatedModels, customModel)

            local rotCFrame = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90), 0, 0)
            local savedProps = {}

            local function SaveProps(h)
                if h:IsA('BasePart') then
                    savedProps[h] = {Size = h.Size}
                elseif h:IsA('SpecialMesh') or h:IsA('BlockMesh') or h:IsA('CylinderMesh') then
                    savedProps[h] = {Scale = h.Scale}
                elseif h:IsA('Attachment') then
                    savedProps[h] = {Position = h.Position}
                elseif h:IsA('ParticleEmitter') then
                    savedProps[h] = {Size = h.Size, Speed = h.Speed}
                elseif h:IsA('Beam') then
                    savedProps[h] = {Width0 = h.Width0, Width1 = h.Width1, CurveSize0 = h.CurveSize0, CurveSize1 = h.CurveSize1}
                elseif h:IsA('Trail') then
                    savedProps[h] = {WidthScale = h.WidthScale}
                elseif h:IsA('PointLight') or h:IsA('SurfaceLight') or h:IsA('SpotLight') then
                    savedProps[h] = {Range = h.Range}
                end
            end

            if customModel:IsA('Model') then
                local boundingCFrame = customModel:GetBoundingBox()
                for _, j in pairs(customModel:GetDescendants()) do
                    if j:IsA('BasePart') then
                        j.Anchored = false
                        j.CanCollide = false
                        local relCFrame = boundingCFrame:ToObjectSpace(j.CFrame)
                        j.CFrame = (d.CFrame * rotCFrame) * relCFrame
                        local weld = Instance.new('WeldConstraint')
                        weld.Part0 = j
                        weld.Part1 = d
                        weld.Parent = j
                        for _, n in pairs(j:GetChildren()) do
                            if n:IsA('Attachment') then
                                n.CFrame = rotCFrame * n.CFrame
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            elseif customModel:IsA('BasePart') then
                customModel.Anchored = false
                customModel.CanCollide = false
                customModel.CFrame = d.CFrame * rotCFrame
                for _, i in pairs(customModel:GetChildren()) do
                    if i:IsA('Attachment') then
                        i.CFrame = rotCFrame * i.CFrame
                    end
                end
                local weld = Instance.new('WeldConstraint')
                weld.Part0 = customModel
                weld.Part1 = d
                weld.Parent = customModel
            end

            local soundPart = customModel:IsA('BasePart') and customModel or customModel:FindFirstChildWhichIsA('BasePart', true)
            local sound
            if soundPart then
                sound = Instance.new('Sound')
                sound.SoundId = 'rbxassetid://71291438346620'
                sound.PlaybackSpeed = 0.5
                sound.Volume = 1.8
                sound.Looped = true
                sound.RollOffMaxDistance = 130
                sound.RollOffMinDistance = 15
                sound.Parent = soundPart
                sound:Play()
            end

            SaveProps(customModel)
            for _, k in pairs(customModel:GetDescendants()) do
                SaveProps(k)
            end

            local isDestroyed, currentScale, shrinkSpeed = false, 1, 1

            local function OnHoleDestroyed()
                if isDestroyed then return end
                isDestroyed = true
                for _, n in pairs(customModel:GetDescendants()) do
                    if n:IsA('BasePart') then n.Anchored = true end
                end
                if customModel:IsA('BasePart') then customModel.Anchored = true end
            end

            table.insert(ActiveConnections, d.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
                if not parent then OnHoleDestroyed() end
            end))

            local heartbeatConn
            heartbeatConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
                if not FeatureEnabled or not customModel or not customModel.Parent then
                    heartbeatConn:Disconnect()
                    return
                end

                if isDestroyed then
                    currentScale = currentScale - (dt * shrinkSpeed)
                    if currentScale <= 0 then
                        currentScale = 0
                        heartbeatConn:Disconnect()
                        customModel:Destroy()
                        return
                    end
                    if sound then sound.Volume = 2 * currentScale end
                end

                local baseScale, generalScale = 0.5 * currentScale, currentScale
                for obj, props in pairs(savedProps) do
                    if obj:IsA('BasePart') then
                        obj.Size = props.Size * baseScale
                    elseif obj:IsA('SpecialMesh') or obj:IsA('BlockMesh') or obj:IsA('CylinderMesh') then
                        obj.Scale = props.Scale * baseScale
                    elseif obj:IsA('Attachment') then
                        obj.Position = props.Position * baseScale
                    elseif obj:IsA('ParticleEmitter') then
                        obj.Size = ScaleNumberSequence(props.Size, generalScale)
                        if props.Speed then obj.Speed = ScaleNumberRange(props.Speed, generalScale) end
                    elseif obj:IsA('Beam') then
                        obj.Width0 = props.Width0 * generalScale
                        obj.Width1 = props.Width1 * generalScale
                        if props.CurveSize0 then
                            obj.CurveSize0 = props.CurveSize0 * generalScale
                            obj.CurveSize1 = props.CurveSize1 * generalScale
                        end
                    elseif obj:IsA('Trail') then
                        obj.WidthScale = ScaleNumberSequence(props.WidthScale, generalScale)
                    elseif obj:IsA('PointLight') or obj:IsA('SurfaceLight') or obj:IsA('SpotLight') then
                        obj.Range = props.Range * generalScale
                    end
                end
            end)
            table.insert(ActiveConnections, heartbeatConn)
        end
    end
end

local function HandleBlackHoleKick(bhk)
    for _, f in pairs(bhk:GetDescendants()) do
        if f.Name == 'Drone' then
            task.defer(function() if f and f.Parent then f:Destroy() end end)
        end
    end
    table.insert(ActiveConnections, bhk.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(e)
        if FeatureEnabled and e.Name == 'Drone' then
            task.defer(function() if e and e.Parent then e:Destroy() end end)
        end
    end))

    for _, f in pairs(bhk:GetDescendants()) do
        ApplyChangesToObj(f)
    end
    table.insert(ActiveConnections, bhk.DescendantAdded:Connect(ApplyChangesToObj))
end


funTab:AddButton({
    Name = "black hole change",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        FeatureEnabled = Value
        if FeatureEnabled then
            for _, e in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if e.Name == 'BlackHoleKick' then
                    HandleBlackHoleKick(e)
                end
            end
            table.insert(ActiveConnections, workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
                if FeatureEnabled and d.Name == 'BlackHoleKick' then
                    HandleBlackHoleKick(d)
                end
            end))
        else
            CleanupCustomizations()
        end
    end    
})




end

local isExecuting = false

local function getMyPlot()
    for i = 1, 5 do
        local plot = workspace.Plots:FindFirstChild("Plot" .. i)
        if plot then
            local plotSign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
            if plotSign then
                local thisPlotsOwners = plotSign:FindFirstChild("ThisPlotsOwners")
                if thisPlotsOwners then
                    for _, child in ipairs(thisPlotsOwners:GetChildren()) do
                        if child:IsA("StringValue") and child.Value == game.Players.LocalPlayer.Name then
                            return plot.PlotArea
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

function PlotBarrierDelete()
if PBDrun then return end
PBDrun = true

local char = plr.Character
if not char then PBDrun = false return end

local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not hrp then PBDrun = false return end

local metal = workspace.Plots.Plot1.TeslaCoil.Metal
if not metal then PBDrun = false return end

local TP = metal.CFrame
local OCF = hrp.CFrame

task.spawn(function()
rs.MenuToys.SpawnToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer("FoodHamburger", hrp.CFrame, Vector3.new(0,0,0))
end)

task.wait(0.2)

local foodHamburger = inv:FindFirstChild("FoodHamburger")
if not foodHamburger then PBDrun = false return end

if foodHamburger then
task.spawn(function()
foodHamburger.HoldPart.HoldItemRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(foodHamburger, char)
end)
end

task.wait(0.1)

hrp.CFrame = TP
task.wait(0.17)

if foodHamburger then
rs.MenuToys.DestroyToy:FireServer(foodHamburger)
end

hrp.CFrame = OCF
task.wait(0.4)

PBDrun = false
end

local Processing = false

local function ExecuteOriginalNoClip(tt, overrideCF)
	if Processing then return end
	Processing = true

	local Players = game:GetService("Players")
	local rs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
	local plr = Players.LocalPlayer

	local char = plr.Character
	if not char then
		Processing = false
		return
	end

	local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local torso = char:FindFirstChild("Torso") or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
	if not hrp or not torso then
		Processing = false
		return
	end

	if not tt or not tt:IsA("BasePart") then 
		Processing = false
		return
	end

	local invFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild(plr.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
	if not invFolder then
		Processing = false
		return
	end

	
	rs.GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(tt, overrideCF)

	task.spawn(function()
		pcall(function()
			rs.MenuToys.SpawnToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer("NinjaShuriken", hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 5, 20), Vector3.zero)
		end)
	end)

	local shuriken = nil
	for i = 1, 30 do
		task.wait(0.1)
		shuriken = invFolder:FindFirstChild("NinjaShuriken")
		if shuriken then break end
	end

	if not shuriken then 
		Processing = false
		return 
	end

	local stickyPart = shuriken:FindFirstChild("StickyPart")
	local soundPart = shuriken:FindFirstChild("SoundPart")

	local isSuccess = false
	if stickyPart and soundPart then
		local ok = pcall(function()
			rs.GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(soundPart, CFrame.lookAt(torso.Position, soundPart.Position))
			task.wait(0.1)
			rs.PlayerEvents.StickyPartEvent:FireServer(stickyPart, tt, CFrame.new(107.5, 9.00000049e+33, -3.86856262e+25, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0.0662516952, -0.997802973, 0, 0.997802973, 0.0662516952))
		end)
		if ok then
			isSuccess = true
		end
	end

	shuriken.Name = ""
	Processing = false

	if isSuccess then
		OrionLib:MakeNotification({
			Name = "[ success ]",
			Content = SelectedHouse .. " destroy barrier",
			Image = "rbxassetid://4483345998",
			Time = 3
		})
	end
end

plotTab:AddButton({
	Name = "Break Outer UFO",
	Callback = function()
		pcall(function()
			local w = Workspace
			local me = LocalPlayer
			local BackPack = w[me.Name .. 'SpawnedInToys']
			
			local ufoModel = w:FindFirstChild("Map")
			if ufoModel then
				ufoModel = ufoModel:FindFirstChild("AlwaysHereTweenedObjects")
				if ufoModel then
					ufoModel = ufoModel:FindFirstChild("OuterUFO")
				end
			end
			
			if not ufoModel then
				OrionLib:MakeNotification({
					Name = "Xeron",
					Content = "Outer UFO not found!",
					Time = 3
				})
				return
			end
			
			local UFO = ufoModel:FindFirstChild("Object")
			if not UFO then
				OrionLib:MakeNotification({
					Name = "Xeron",
					Content = "UFO Object not found!",
					Time = 3
				})
				return
			end
			
			local body = UFO:FindFirstChild("ObjectModel")
			if body then
				body = body:FindFirstChild("Body")
			end
			
			if not body then
				OrionLib:MakeNotification({
					Name = "Xeron",
					Content = "UFO Body not found!",
					Time = 3
				})
				return
			end
			
			local function spawnShurikens(count)
				local spawnCFrame = me.Character and me.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not spawnCFrame then return end
				spawnCFrame = spawnCFrame.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 3, 0)
				
				for i = 1, count do
					pcall(function()
						ReplicatedStorage.MenuToys.SpawnToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(
							"NinjaShuriken",
							spawnCFrame,
							Vector3.zero
						)
					end)
					task.wait(0.05)
				end
			end
			
			spawnShurikens(10)
			task.wait(1)
			
			for i = 1, 10 do
				local shur = BackPack and BackPack:FindFirstChild("NinjaShuriken")
				if shur then
					local stickyPart = shur:FindFirstChild("StickyPart")
					if stickyPart then
						shur.Name = tostring(i)
						pcall(function()
							ReplicatedStorage.PlayerEvents.StickyPartEvent:FireServer(
								stickyPart,
								body,
								CFrame.Angles(0, 0, 0)
							)
						end)
					end
				end
				task.wait(0.1)
			end
			
			for i = 1, 100 do
				pcall(function()
					ReplicatedStorage.GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(body, body.CFrame)
				end)
				task.wait()
			end
			
			local attach = body:FindFirstChild("ObjectModelAttachment")
			if attach then
				attach:Destroy()
			end
			
			local followPart = ufoModel:FindFirstChild("FollowThisPart")
			if followPart then
				local alignPos = followPart:FindFirstChild("AlignPosition")
				local alignRot = followPart:FindFirstChild("AlignOrientation")
				
				if alignPos then
					alignPos.Attachment0 = nil
				end
				if alignRot then
					alignRot.Attachment0 = nil
				end
			end
			
			OrionLib:MakeNotification({
				Name = "Xeron",
				Content = "Outer UFO Broken!",
				Time = 3
			})
		end)
	end
})
