do

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")

local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

local PcldViewT = false
local processed = {}
local redBoxes = {}
local descendantConn = nil


function PcldViewF()
    if descendantConn then
        descendantConn:Disconnect()
        descendantConn = nil
        workspace:SetAttribute("PcldViewDescendantConn", false)
    end
    if rainbowConn then
        rainbowConn:Disconnect()
        rainbowConn = nil
    end

    if PcldViewT then

        local function createESP(obj)
            if not processed[obj] then
                local box = Instance.new("SelectionBox")
                box.Adornee = obj
                box.LineThickness = 0.005
                box.SurfaceTransparency = 1
                box.SurfaceColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                box.Color3 = Color3.fromHSV(0, 1, 1) -- 초기 색상 (빨간색)
                box.Parent = obj
                processed[obj] = true
                redBoxes[obj] = box
            end
        end

        
        for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
            if (obj.Name == "PlayerCharacterLocationDetector" or obj.Name == "me") and obj:IsA("BasePart") then
                createESP(obj)
            end
        end

    
        descendantConn = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
            if PcldViewT and (obj.Name == "PlayerCharacterLocationDetector" or obj.Name == "me") and obj:IsA("BasePart") then
                createESP(obj)
            end
        end)

        
        workspace.DescendantRemoving:Connect(function(obj)
            if redBoxes[obj] then
                redBoxes[obj]:Destroy()
                redBoxes[obj] = nil
                processed[obj] = nil
            end
        end)

        
        rainbowConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
            
            local hue = (tick() % 5) / 5 
            local rainbowColor = Color3.fromHSV(hue, 1, 1)

            for obj, box in pairs(redBoxes) do
                if box and box.Parent then
                    box.Color3 = rainbowColor
                else
                    redBoxes[obj] = nil
                    processed[obj] = nil
                end
            end
        end)

    else
                for obj, box in pairs(redBoxes) do
            if box then box:Destroy() end
        end
        redBoxes = {}
        processed = {}
    end
end


keybindTab:AddSection({
	Name = "ESP"
})

keybindTab:AddToggle({
    Name = "PCLD view",
    Default = false,
    save = true,
    Flag = "PCLDView",
    Callback = function(Value)
        PcldViewT = Value
        PcldViewF()
    end
})

keybindTab:AddToggle({
    Name = "Part View",
    Default = false,
    save = true,
    Flag = "ViewTool",
    Callback = function(Value)
        ViewToolT = Value
        ViewToolF()
    end
})
end

DDTab:AddSection({
    Name = "Emote"
})
        DDTab:AddToggle({
    Name = " Tea bagging  <font color='rgb(25,255,0)'>[Taebaegging]</font>",
    CurrentValue= false,
    Flag = "JerkMotionToggle",
    Callback = function(Value)
        jerkActive = Value

        local player = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
        local character = player.Character
        if not character then return end

        local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not humanoid then return end

        local animator = humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
        if not animator then
            animator = Instance.new("Animator")
            animator.Parent = humanoid
        end

        if jerkActive then
            if not jerkAnimationTrack then
                local animation = Instance.new("Animation")
                animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://168268306"
                jerkAnimationTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(animation)
            end

            jerkAnimationTrack:Play()
            task.wait(0.3)

            task.spawn(function()
                while jerkActive and jerkAnimationTrack and jerkAnimationTrack.IsPlaying do
                    jerkAnimationTrack.TimePosition = 0.3
                    task.wait(0.1)
                end
            end)
        else
            if jerkAnimationTrack then
                jerkAnimationTrack:Stop()
            end
        end
    end
})

do
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedFirst = game:GetService("ReplicatedFirst")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer



local SelectedAnimation = "Crouch"
local AnimationToggle = false
local CurrentAnimTrack = nil

local AnimPaths = {
    ["Crouch"] = ReplicatedFirst.Animations.Crouch,
    ["FireFlailAnimation"] = ReplicatedFirst.CatchFire.FireFlailAnimation,
    ["Flail"] = ReplicatedFirst.ThrowPlayers.Flail,
    ["Type"] = ReplicatedFirst.Typing.Type
}

local AnimationList = {"Crouch", "FireFlailAnimation", "Flail", "Type"}

 function stopAllTracks()
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if humanoid then
        for _, track in pairs(humanoid:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
            track:Stop()
        end
    end
end

function playSelectedAnimation()
    if not AnimationToggle then return end

    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not humanoid then return end

    local animObject = AnimPaths[SelectedAnimation]
    if animObject and animObject:IsA("Animation") then
        stopAllTracks()
        CurrentAnimTrack = humanoid:LoadAnimation(animObject)
        CurrentAnimTrack.Looped = true
        CurrentAnimTrack:Play()
    end
end

DDTab:AddDropdown({
    Name = "Animation",
    Options = AnimationList,
    CurrentOption = "Crouch",
    MultipleOptions = false,
    Flag = "SelectedAnim",
    Callback = function(Option)
        if typeof(Option) == "table" then
            SelectedAnimation = Option[1] or "Crouch"
        else
            SelectedAnimation = Option
        end

        if AnimationToggle then
            playSelectedAnimation()
        end
    end
})

DDTab:AddToggle({
    Name = "Play Animation",
    CurrentValue = false,
    Flag = "AnimToggle",
    Callback = function(Value)
        AnimationToggle = Value
        if Value then
            playSelectedAnimation()
        else
            stopAllTracks()
            if CurrentAnimTrack then
                CurrentAnimTrack:Stop()
                CurrentAnimTrack = nil
            end
        end
    end
})

LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
    if AnimationToggle then
        local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
        if humanoid then
            task.wait(1)
            playSelectedAnimation()
        end
    end
end)

                for i = 1, tonumber(LineLagV) do
                    game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").GrabEvents.CreateGrabLine:FireServer(unpack(args))
                end
            end)
            task.wait() 
        end
    end)
end

local function disconnectConnection(connection)
    if connection then
        if type(connection) == "userdata" and connection.Disconnect then
            connection:Disconnect()
        elseif type(connection) == "thread" then
            task.cancel(connection)
        end
    end
end

                while getgenv().crazyLineRunning do
                    local randomPart = workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Part")
                    if randomPart then
                        pcall(function()
                            game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").GrabEvents.CreateGrabLine:FireServer(
                                randomPart,
                                CFrame.new(
                                    math.random(-100, 100),
                                    math.random(-100, 100),
                                    math.random(-100, 100)
                                )
                            )
                        end)
                    end
                    task.wait(0.1)
                end
            end)
        else
            getgenv().crazyLineRunning = false
        end
    end
})

do
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

local spawnedInToysFolder = Workspace:WaitForChild(localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys", 5) or Workspace:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
if not spawnedInToysFolder then
    return
end

local toysLimitCapValue = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("ToysLimitCap") or localPlayer:WaitForChild("ToysLimitCap", 5)
local usedToyPointsValue = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("UsedToyPoints") or localPlayer:WaitForChild("UsedToyPoints", 5)
if not (toysLimitCapValue and usedToyPointsValue) then
    return
end

local menuToys = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("MenuToys") or ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("MenuToys", 5)
local grabEvents = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("GrabEvents") or ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("GrabEvents", 5)
local BombEvents = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("BombEvents") or ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("BombEvents", 5)
if not (menuToys and grabEvents and BombEvents) then
    return
end

local SpawnToyRF = menuToys:FindFirstChild("SpawnToyRemoteFunction") or menuToys:WaitForChild("SpawnToyRemoteFunction", 5)
local DeleteToyRE = menuToys:FindFirstChild("DestroyToy") or menuToys:WaitForChild("DestroyToy", 5)
local setNetworkOwnerEvent = grabEvents:FindFirstChild("SetNetworkOwner") or grabEvents:WaitForChild("SetNetworkOwner", 5)
if not (SpawnToyRF and DeleteToyRE and setNetworkOwnerEvent) then
    return
end

local spamexplosiontype = "FireworkMissile"
local spamexplosiontarget = 0
local bombsammountoexplode = 1
local explosionInterval = 0
local FireworkEffectSpam = false
local TriggerMode = 1
local ExplodingBombs = false
local PredictPlayer = false
local TargetToBombPlayer = ""
local FireBomb = false
local OnDisableAction = "destroy"

local heartbeatConnection = nil
local spamCooldown = false
local lastExplosionTime = 0

local explosionTypeMap = {
    ["Firecracker"] = "FireworkMissile",
    ["Missile"] = "BombMissile",
    ["Black hole"] = "BombDarkMatter",
    ["balloon"] = "BombBalloon",
    ["small gift"] = "PresentSmall",
    ["big gift"] = "PresentBig"
}

local bombHitboxMap = {
    BombMissile = "PartHitDetector",
    BombDarkMatter = "PartHitDetector",
    FireworkMissile = "PartHitDetector",
    BombBalloon = "Balloon",
    PresentBig = "Box",
    PresentSmall = "Box"
}

local cleanupTypes = {
    "FireworkMissile",
    "BombMissile",
    "BombDarkMatter",
    "BombBalloon",
    "PresentSmall",
    "PresentBig"
}

 function DeleteAllBombs()
    for _, bomb in pairs(spawnedInToysFolder:GetChildren()) do
        for _, bombType in pairs(cleanupTypes) do
            if bomb.Name == bombType then
                pcall(function()
                    DeleteToyRE:FireServer(bomb)
                end)
                break
            end
        end
    end
end

function CleanupCurrentType()
    local currentBombType = spamexplosiontype
    for _, bomb in pairs(spawnedInToysFolder:GetChildren()) do
        if bomb.Name == currentBombType then
            pcall(function()
                DeleteToyRE:FireServer(bomb)
            end)
        end
    end
end

function GetPlayerCFrame()
    if localPlayer and localPlayer.Character then
        local hrp = localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if hrp then
            return hrp.CFrame
        end
    end
    return CFrame.new()
end

function CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(potentialPart)
    if potentialPart and potentialPart:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") then
        return potentialPart.PartOwner.Value == localPlayer.Name
    end
    return false
end

function SNOWshipOnce(targetPart)
    if not targetPart then return false end
    local distanceFromCharacter = localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(targetPart.Position)
    if localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
        if CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(targetPart) then
            return true
        end
        if distanceFromCharacter <= 30 then
            setNetworkOwnerEvent:FireServer(targetPart, CFrame.new(localPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position, targetPart.Position))
        end
    end
    return false
end

function CheckToyLimit()
    return (usedToyPointsValue.Value + 10) / toysLimitCapValue.Value > 1
end

 function countToys(toyName)
    local toyCount = 0
    for _, child in pairs(spawnedInToysFolder:GetChildren()) do
        if child.Name == toyName then
            toyCount = toyCount + 1
        end
    end
    return toyCount
end

function ExplodeBomb(bombInstance, positionPart, otherData)
    local hitboxName = bombHitboxMap[bombInstance.Name] or "Hitbox"
    local hitbox = bombInstance:FindFirstChild(hitboxName)
    if hitbox then
        local bombExplosionData = {
            {
                Hitbox = hitbox,
                PositionPart = positionPart
            },
            otherData
        }
        BombEvents.BombExplode:FireServer(unpack(bombExplosionData))
    end
end

local function ExplodeByTargetMode(bombInstance)
    if spamexplosiontarget == 0 then
        ExplodeBomb(bombInstance, workspace.SpawnLocation, Vector3.new(math.random(-10, 10), math.random(-10, 10), math.random(-10, 10)))
    elseif spamexplosiontarget == 1 then
        local targetPlayer = Players:FindFirstChild(TargetToBombPlayer)
        if targetPlayer and targetPlayer.Character and targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            local hrp = targetPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart
            if PredictPlayer then
                ExplodeBomb(bombInstance, hrp, hrp.Position + hrp.Velocity / 1.93)
            else
                ExplodeBomb(bombInstance, hrp, hrp.Position)
            end
        end
    end
end

function ExplodeFirstBomb()
    local targetPart = spawnedInToysFolder:FindFirstChild(spamexplosiontype)
    if targetPart then
        ExplodeByTargetMode(targetPart)
    end
end

function ExplodeAllAtOnce()
    if ExplodingBombs then return end
    ExplodingBombs = true
    
    local currentTime = tick()
    if currentTime - lastExplosionTime < 0.1 then
        ExplodingBombs = false
        return
    end
    
    lastExplosionTime = currentTime
    
    local snapshot = {}
    for _, targetInstance in pairs(spawnedInToysFolder:GetChildren()) do
        if targetInstance.Name == spamexplosiontype then
            table.insert(snapshot, targetInstance)
        end
    end
    
    for _, targetInstance in pairs(snapshot) do
        if targetInstance.Parent then
            ExplodeByTargetMode(targetInstance)
            if explosionInterval > 0 then
                task.wait(explosionInterval)
            end
        end
    end
    
    task.wait(0.1)
    ExplodingBombs = false
end

function HandleSpamLogic()
    if not FireworkEffectSpam or spamCooldown then return end
    
    local playerCFrame = GetPlayerCFrame()
    if not playerCFrame then return end
    
    if not CheckToyLimit() and not ExplodingBombs then
        local toyCount = countToys(spamexplosiontype)
        
        if toyCount < bombsammountoexplode then
            SpawnToyRF:InvokeServer(spamexplosiontype, playerCFrame, Vector3.new(0, 97.69, 0))
        end
        
        if TriggerMode == 1 and toyCount >= bombsammountoexplode then
            spamCooldown = true
            ExplodeAllAtOnce()
            task.wait(0.2)
            spamCooldown = false
        end
    end
end

function HandleCleanupLogic()
    if not FireworkEffectSpam then return end
    
    for _, child in pairs(spawnedInToysFolder:GetChildren()) do
        if child.Name == spamexplosiontype then
            local hitboxPart = nil
            if spamexplosiontype == "BombDarkMatter" then
                hitboxPart = child:FindFirstChild("Pyramid")
            elseif spamexplosiontype == "BombMissile" then
                hitboxPart = child:FindFirstChild("Body")
            elseif spamexplosiontype == "BombBalloon" then
                hitboxPart = child:FindFirstChild("Balloon")
            elseif spamexplosiontype == "FireworkMissile" then
                hitboxPart = child:FindFirstChild("Hitbox")
            elseif spamexplosiontype == "PresentBig" or spamexplosiontype == "PresentSmall" then
                hitboxPart = child:FindFirstChild("Box")
            end
            
            if hitboxPart then
                if not SNOWshipOnce(hitboxPart) and localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(hitboxPart.Position) > 30 then
                    DeleteToyRE:FireServer(child)
                elseif CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(hitboxPart) and not child:GetAttribute("MissileTeleported") then
                    local stableBodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
                    stableBodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                    stableBodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1) * math.huge
                    stableBodyVelocity.Name = "Stable"
                    stableBodyVelocity.Parent = child.PrimaryPart
                    
                    task.wait()
                    child:PivotTo(CFrame.new(math.random(-1000, 1000), 10000, math.random(-1000, 1000)))
                    child:SetAttribute("MissileTeleported", true)
                    
                    task.wait(0.1)
                    if stableBodyVelocity then
                        stableBodyVelocity:Destroy()
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end
function HandleExplosionLogic()
    if not FireworkEffectSpam then return end
    
    if TriggerMode == 0 and FireBomb then
        ExplodeFirstBomb()
        FireBomb = false
    end
end

 function SetupHeartbeat()
    if heartbeatConnection then
        heartbeatConnection:Disconnect()
        heartbeatConnection = nil
    end
    
    heartbeatConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        HandleSpamLogic()
        HandleCleanupLogic()
        HandleExplosionLogic()
    end)
end
 function refreshPlayerList(uiElement)
    local playerNamesList = {}
    for _, player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player.UserId ~= localPlayer.UserId then
            table.insert(playerNamesList, player.Name .. " (@" .. player.DisplayName .. ")")
        end
    end
    table.sort(playerNamesList, function(a, b) return a:lower() < b:lower() end)
    --uiElement:Refresh(playerNamesList, true)
end

SMTab:AddSection({
    Name = "Explosive spam"
})

SMTab:AddToggle({
    Name = "Explosive ",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(fireworkEffectSpamToggle)
        FireworkEffectSpam = fireworkEffectSpamToggle
        spamCooldown = false
        ExplodingBombs = false
        
        if FireworkEffectSpam then
            if not heartbeatConnection then
                SetupHeartbeat()
            end
        else
            if heartbeatConnection then
                heartbeatConnection:Disconnect()
                heartbeatConnection = nil
            end
            
            if OnDisableAction == "destroy" then
                DeleteAllBombs()
            end
        end
    end
})

        "Firework",
        "Missile",
        "Blackhole",
        "Balloon",
        "Small Gift",
        "Big Gift"
    },

    Callback = function(explosionType)
        local oldType = spamexplosiontype
        spamexplosiontype = explosionTypeMap[explosionType] or "FireworkMissile"
        
        if oldType ~= spamexplosiontype then
            CleanupCurrentType()
        end
    end
})

    Name = "On Toggle Off",

local character = plr.Character or plr.CharacterAdded:Wait()
local rootPart = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)

_G.CameraLoopID = (_G.CameraLoopID or 0) + 1
local currentLoopID = _G.CameraLoopID

task.spawn(function()
    plr.CameraMode = Enum.CameraMode.Classic
    task.wait(0.05)

    local success = false
    local attempts = 0

    while _G.CameraLoopID == currentLoopID and not success and attempts < 80 do
        plr.CameraMinZoomDistance = 12
        plr.CameraMaxZoomDistance = 12

        task.wait(0.05)
        attempts = attempts + 1

        if plr:GetAttribute("CameraZoomDistance") == 12
        or (workspace.CurrentCamera
        and rootPart
        and (workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position - rootPart.Position).Magnitude >= 11.5) then
            success = true
        end
    end

    if _G.CameraLoopID == currentLoopID then
        plr.CameraMinZoomDistance = 0.5
        plr.CameraMaxZoomDistance = 100000
    end
end)

do
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local StarterGui = game:GetService("StarterGui")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

local plr = Players.LocalPlayer

local GrabEvents = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("GrabEvents", 5)
if not GrabEvents then
    warn("GrabEvents")
    return
end

local CreateGrabLine = GrabEvents:WaitForChild("CreateGrabLine", 5)
if not CreateGrabLine then
    warn("CreateGrabLine")
    return
end

local Pline = CreateGrabLine.OnClientEvent

if not getgenv()._AntiLagScript then
    getgenv()._AntiLagScript = {
        antiLagEnabled = true,
        antiLagMode = true,
        AntiLagV = 20,
        MinFPS = 60,
        FrameBurstLimit = 3,
        RapidWindow = 0.2,
        RapidLimit = 10,
        fireCount = {},
        isLagging = false,
        isFpsLow = false,
        suspectPlayer = nil,
        connection = nil,
        globalFrame = 0,
        pendingResetFrame = {},
        charBeamMove = nil,
        renderConnection = nil,
        loopConnection = nil
    }
end

local AL = getgenv()._AntiLagScript
AL.charBeamMove = plr:WaitForChild("PlayerScripts"):WaitForChild("CharacterAndBeamMove", 5)

local function UpdateCharBeamState()
    if not AL.charBeamMove then return end

    if not AL.antiLagEnabled then
        AL.charBeamMove.Enabled = true
        return
    end

    if not AL.antiLagMode then
        AL.charBeamMove.Enabled = false
        return
    end

    if AL.isLagging or AL.isFpsLow then
        AL.charBeamMove.Enabled = false
    else
        AL.charBeamMove.Enabled = true
    end
end

function FlagPlayer(fromPlr)
    local data = AL.fireCount[fromPlr]
    if not data then return end
    if data.isFlagged then return end

    data.isFlagged = true

    if not AL.isLagging then
        AL.isLagging = true
        AL.suspectPlayer = fromPlr

        UpdateCharBeamState()

        pcall(function()
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                Title = "[ lag ]",
                Text = fromPlr.Name .. " [ " .. fromPlr.DisplayName .. " ]",
                Duration = 5
            })
        end)
    else
        pcall(function()
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                Title = "[ lag ]",
                Text = fromPlr.Name .. " [ " .. fromPlr.DisplayName .. " ]",
                Duration = 3
            })
        end)
    end
end

AL.renderConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    AL.globalFrame += 1

    for plrRef, resetFrame in pairs(AL.pendingResetFrame) do
        if resetFrame < AL.globalFrame then
            local data = AL.fireCount[plrRef]
            if data then
                data.frameBurst = 0
            end
            AL.pendingResetFrame[plrRef] = nil
        end
    end
end)

AL.loopConnection = task.spawn(function()
    local frames = 0
    local frameCounter = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if AL.antiLagEnabled then
            frames += 1
        end
    end)

    while AL.antiLagEnabled do
        task.wait(1)
        local currentFPS = frames
        frames = 0
        
        local anyLagging = false

        for fromPlr, data in pairs(AL.fireCount) do
            data.cps = data.count
            data.count = 0
            
            if data.isFlagged then
                if data.cps <= 10 then
                    data.isFlagged = false
                else
                    anyLagging = true
                    AL.suspectPlayer = fromPlr
                end
            end
        end

        AL.isLagging = anyLagging
        if not AL.isLagging then
            AL.suspectPlayer = nil
        end

        if currentFPS < AL.MinFPS then
            if not AL.isFpsLow then
                AL.isFpsLow = true
                UpdateCharBeamState()
            end
        else
            AL.isFpsLow = false
        end
    end
    
    frameCounter:Disconnect()
end)

function AntiLagF()
    if AL.connection then
        AL.connection:Disconnect()
        AL.connection = nil
    end

    UpdateCharBeamState()

    AL.connection = Pline:Connect(function(fromPlr, ...)
        if typeof(fromPlr) ~= "Instance" or not fromPlr:IsA("Player") then return end
        if fromPlr == plr then return end

        if not AL.antiLagEnabled or not AL.antiLagMode then return end

        if not AL.fireCount[fromPlr] then
            AL.fireCount[fromPlr] = {
                count = 0,
                cps = 0,
                lastCallTime = 0,
                isFlagged = false,
                lastFrame = 0,
                frameBurst = 0,
                rapidWindowStart = 0,
                rapidCount = 0
            }
        end

        local data = AL.fireCount[fromPlr]
        local currentTime = os.clock()

        if data.lastFrame == AL.globalFrame then
            data.frameBurst += 1

            if data.frameBurst >= AL.FrameBurstLimit then
                FlagPlayer(fromPlr)
            end
        else
            data.lastFrame = AL.globalFrame
            data.frameBurst = 1
            AL.pendingResetFrame[fromPlr] = AL.globalFrame
        end

        if currentTime - data.rapidWindowStart > AL.RapidWindow then
            data.rapidWindowStart = currentTime
            data.rapidCount = 1
        else
            data.rapidCount += 1

            if data.rapidCount >= AL.RapidLimit then
                FlagPlayer(fromPlr)
            end
        end

        if data.lastCallTime ~= 0 and (currentTime - data.lastCallTime) <= 0.025 then
            data.count = AL.AntiLagV
        end

        data.lastCallTime = currentTime
        data.count += 1

        if data.count >= AL.AntiLagV then
            FlagPlayer(fromPlr)
        end
    end)
end

AntiLagF()

end
do
--[[
__uiSafeBlock("UIcusTab", function()
	ColorsSection = UIcusTab:AddSection({
		Name = "Interface Colors"
	})

 function ReturnProperty(Object)
    if not Object or not Object.ClassName then
        return "BackgroundColor3"
    end
    
    local className = Object.ClassName
    
    if className == "Frame" or className == "TextButton" then
        return "BackgroundColor3"
    elseif className == "ScrollingFrame" then
        return "ScrollBarImageColor3"
    elseif className == "UIStroke" then
        return "Color"
    elseif className == "TextLabel" or className == "TextBox" then
        return "TextColor3"
    elseif className == "ImageLabel" or className == "ImageButton" then
        return "ImageColor3"
    else
        return "BackgroundColor3"
    end
end

 function UpdateSpecificTheme(Type, Value)
    OrionLib.Themes[OrionLib.SelectedTheme][Type] = Value
    
    for _, object in ipairs(OrionLib.ThemeObjects[Type] or {}) do
        if object and object.Parent then
            local property = ReturnProperty(object)
            pcall(function()
                if object[property] then
                    object[property] = Value
                end
            end)
        end
    end
end

ColorsSection:AddColorpicker({
    Name = "메인색",
    Default = OrionLib.Themes[OrionLib.SelectedTheme].Main,
    Flag = "MainColor",
    Save = true,
    Callback = function(Value)
        UpdateSpecificTheme("Main", Value)
    end    
})

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")

local plr = Players.LocalPlayer
local mouse = plr:GetMouse()

local function tpF()
    local char = plr.Character
    if not char then return end

    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return end

    local hum = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
    if not hum then return end

    local ragdolled = hum:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")

    local target = mouse.Target
    if not target then return end

    local currentCFrame = hrp.CFrame
    local currentRotation = CFrame.new(Vector3.zero, currentCFrame.LookVector)

    local parts = {"Head", "Torso", "Left Leg", "Right Leg", "Left Arm", "Right Arm"}
    for _, name in ipairs(parts) do
        local part = char:FindFirstChild(name)
        if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
            part.Transparency = 0
        end
    end

    for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
        if obj:IsA("Accessory") and obj.Name ~= "TypingKeyboardMyWorld" then
            for _, v in ipairs(obj:GetChildren()) do
                if v:IsA("BasePart") then
                    v.Transparency = 0
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local mouseHit = mouse.Hit
    local targetPos = mouseHit.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
    local newCFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos) * currentRotation

    if ragdolled and ragdolled.Value == true then
        char:PivotTo(newCFrame)
    else
        hrp.CFrame = newCFrame
    end
end

UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
    if gameProcessed then return end
    if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Z then
        tpF()
    end
end)

local Stats = game:GetService("Stats")

spawn(function()
    while task.wait(1) do
        local ping = tonumber(string.split(Stats.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValueString(), " ")[1])

        if ping >= 1000 then
            OrionLib:MakeNotification({
                Name = "warning",
                Content = "The ping is very high! ("..ping..")",
                Time = 3
            })
        elseif ping >= 610 then
            OrionLib:MakeNotification({
                Name = "warning",
                Content = "The ping is high! ("..ping..")",
                Time = 3
            })
        end
    end
end)

end
do
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
local SpawnRemote = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("MenuToys"):WaitForChild("SpawnToyRemoteFunction")
local NetOwnerRemote = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("GrabEvents"):WaitForChild("SetNetworkOwner")
local DestroyRemote = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("MenuToys"):WaitForChild("DestroyToy")

local pallets = {}
local wingConnection = nil
local isWingsActive = false

local palletCount = 12
local wingSpeed = 4
local wingAngle = 15

local isWaveActive = false
local isWaveV2Active = false
local waveFunctionStr = "Sin"
local waveIntensity = 1

local waveFuncs = {
	["Sin"] = math.sin,
	["Cos"] = math.cos,
	["Tan"] = math.tan
}

local function getTargetPart(obj)
	if not obj or typeof(obj) ~= "Instance" then return nil end
	if obj:IsA("BasePart") then return obj end
	local soundPart = obj:FindFirstChild("SoundPart", true)
	if soundPart and soundPart:IsA("BasePart") then return soundPart end
	return obj.PrimaryPart or obj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
end

local function claimOwnership(target)
	local part = getTargetPart(target)
	if part then
		for _ = 1, 3 do
			pcall(function() NetOwnerRemote:FireServer(part, part.CFrame) end)
			task.wait()
		end
	end
end

 function spawnPallet()
	local char = LocalPlayer.Character
	local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not hrp then return nil end

	local spawnCF = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 5, 0)
	local newlyAdded = {}
	local conn = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(desc) table.insert(newlyAdded, desc) end)

	local result = SpawnRemote:InvokeServer("PalletLightBrown", spawnCF, Vector3.new(0, 90, 0))
	task.wait(0.05)
	conn:Disconnect()

	local spawnedObj = nil

	if typeof(result) == "Instance" then
		spawnedObj = result
	elseif type(result) == "string" then
		spawnedObj = workspace:FindFirstChild(result, true)
		if not spawnedObj and getnilinstances then
			for _, obj in getnilinstances() do
				if obj.Name == result then spawnedObj = obj; break end
			end
		end
	end

	if not spawnedObj then
		for _, obj in ipairs(newlyAdded) do
			if obj:IsA("Model") and (obj.Name == "PalletLightBrown" or obj.Name:find("Pallet") or obj.Name == result) then
				spawnedObj = obj
				break
			end
		end
	end

	if spawnedObj then
		claimOwnership(spawnedObj)
		return spawnedObj
	end
	return nil
end

 function destroyPallet(pallet)
	if not pallet then return end
	pcall(function()
		DestroyRemote:FireServer(pallet)
	end)
	if pallet and pallet.Parent then
		pallet:Destroy()
	end
end
function stopWings()
	if wingConnection then
		wingConnection:Disconnect()
		wingConnection = nil
	end

	for _, pallet in ipairs(pallets) do
		destroyPallet(pallet)
	end
	pallets = {}
end

 function startWings()
	stopWings()

	local lastNetUpdate = 0

	wingConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		local char = LocalPlayer.Character
		if not char or not char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then return end

		local hrp = char.HumanoidRootPart
		local t = tick()

		local shouldUpdateNetwork = false
		if t - lastNetUpdate > 0.5 then
			shouldUpdateNetwork = true
			lastNetUpdate = t
		end

		local halfCount = math.ceil(#pallets / 2)
		local leftChainCF, rightChainCF

		for idx, pallet in ipairs(pallets) do
			local part = getTargetPart(pallet)
			
			if part then
				local isRight = idx > halfCount
				local side = isRight and 1 or -1
				local wingIdx = isRight and (idx - halfCount) or idx

				local currentWave
				if isWaveActive or isWaveV2Active then
					local wFn = waveFuncs[waveFunctionStr] or math.sin
					local phase = (t * wingSpeed) + (wingIdx * waveIntensity)
					currentWave = wFn(phase)
					if waveFunctionStr == "Tan" then
						currentWave = math.clamp(currentWave, -1.5, 1.5)
					end
				else
					currentWave = math.sin(t * wingSpeed)
				end

				local roll = math.rad((isRight and wingAngle or -wingAngle) * currentWave)
				local targetCFrame

				if isWaveV2Active then
					if isRight then
						if wingIdx == 1 then
							rightChainCF = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(15, 1, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, roll)
							targetCFrame = rightChainCF
						else
							rightChainCF = rightChainCF * CFrame.new(11.4, 0, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, roll)
							targetCFrame = rightChainCF
						end
					else
						if wingIdx == 1 then
							leftChainCF = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(-15, 1, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, roll)
							targetCFrame = leftChainCF
						else
							leftChainCF = leftChainCF * CFrame.new(-11.4, 0, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, roll)
							targetCFrame = leftChainCF
						end
					end
				else
					local pivot = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 1, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, roll)
					local xOffset = side * (15 + (wingIdx - 1) * 11.4)
					targetCFrame = pivot * CFrame.new(xOffset, 1, 0)
				end

				part.CanCollide = false
				part.Velocity = Vector3.zero
				part.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero

				if typeof(pallet) == "Instance" and pallet:IsA("Model") then
					pallet:PivotTo(targetCFrame)
				else
					part.CFrame = targetCFrame
				end

				if shouldUpdateNetwork then
					pcall(function() NetOwnerRemote:FireServer(part, targetCFrame) end)
				end
			end
		end
	end)

	task.spawn(function()
		for i = 1, palletCount do
			if not isWingsActive then break end
			local pallet = spawnPallet()
			if pallet then 
				table.insert(pallets, pallet) 
			end
			task.wait()
		end
	end)
end

WTab:AddSection({
	Name = "Wing"
})

WTab:AddToggle({
	Name = "wing",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(Value)
		isWingsActive = Value
		if isWingsActive then
			startWings()
		else
			stopWings()
		end
	end    
})

WTab:AddSlider({
	Name = "number",
	Min = 2,
	Max = 20,
	Default = 12,
	Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
	Increment = 2,
	ValueName = "Number",
	Callback = function(Value)
		palletCount = Value
		if isWingsActive then
			startWings()
		end
	end    
})

WTab:AddSlider({
	Name = "Wing speed",
	Min = 1,
	Max = 20,
	Default = 4,
	Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
	Increment = 1,
	ValueName = "Speed",
	Callback = function(Value)
		wingSpeed = Value
	end    
})

	Max = 90,
	Default = 15,
	Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
	Increment = 1,

		wingAngle = Value
	end    
})

WTab:AddToggle({
	Name = "wave mode (V1: standalone type)",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(Value)
		isWaveActive = Value
		if Value and isWaveV2Active then
			isWaveV2Active = false
		end
	end    
})

	Name = "Wave V2 (connected type)",
	Default = false,

		if Value and isWaveActive then
			isWaveActive = false
		end
	end    
})

local WaveShapeLabel = WTab:AddLabel("shape: 〰〰〰 (Soft waves)")

WTab:AddDropdown({
	Name = "Wave function select",
	Default = "Sin",
	Options = {"Sin", "Cos", "Tan"},
	Callback = function(Value)
		waveFunctionStr = Value
		if Value == "Sin" then
			WaveShapeLabel:Set("shape: 〰〰〰 ")
		elseif Value == "Cos" then
			WaveShapeLabel:Set("shape: ∿∿∿")
		elseif Value == "Tan" then
			WaveShapeLabel:Set("shape: ⭡⭣⭡⭣ ")
		end
	end    
})

WTab:AddSlider({
	Name = "Wave strength",
	Min = 1,
	Max = 100,
	Default = 20,
	Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
	Increment = 1,
	ValueName = "strength",
	Callback = function(Value)
		waveIntensity = Value / 20
	end    
})

do
local SelectedTarget = "UFO 1"

local Targets = {
	["UFO 1"] = {
		GetPart = function() return workspace:WaitForChild("Map"):WaitForChild("AlwaysHereTweenedObjects"):WaitForChild("OuterUFO"):WaitForChild("Object"):WaitForChild("ObjectModel"):WaitForChild("Hitbox") end,
		CF = CFrame.new(-5.893322944641113, 124.04673767089844, -365.1640319824219, 0.5838075876235962, 0.6768856644630432, -0.44832420349121094, 0, 0.5521968007087708, 0.8337138295173645, 0.8118920922279358, -0.4867284595966339, 0.32237666845321655)

		CF = CFrame.new(-350.8216552734375, -2.2597317695617676, 437.3183898925781, -0.6960304379463196, 0.6988924741744995, -0.16459305584430695, 0, 0.22923432290554047, 0.9733712673187256, 0.7180122137069702, 0.6774960160255432, -0.15955407917499542)

	Options = {"UFO 1", "UFO 2", "mine train"},
	Callback = function(Value)
		SelectedTarget = Value
	end

local isToggled = false

WTab:AddToggle({
	Name = "neutralization",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(Value)
		isToggled = Value
		
		if isToggled then
			task.spawn(function()
				local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
				local Players = game:GetService("Players")
				local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
				local plr = Players.LocalPlayer
				
				local character = plr.Character or plr.CharacterAdded:Wait()
				local hrp = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				local torso = character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
				if not torso then return end

				local StickyPartEvent = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("PlayerEvents"):WaitForChild("StickyPartEvent")
				local SpawnToy = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("MenuToys"):WaitForChild("SpawnToyRemoteFunction")
				local SetNetworkOwner = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("GrabEvents"):WaitForChild("SetNetworkOwner")

				local targetInfo = Targets[SelectedTarget]
				if not targetInfo then return end
				local targetPart = targetInfo.GetPart()

				if not targetPart then return end

				local function GetPlotNumber()
					local char = plr.Character
					if not char then return nil end

					if char.Parent == workspace then 
						return nil
					elseif char.Parent.Name == "PlayersInPlots" then
						for _, plot in workspace.Plots:GetChildren() do
							local plotSign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
							if plotSign and plotSign:FindFirstChild("ThisPlotsOwners") then
								for _, owner in plotSign.ThisPlotsOwners:GetChildren() do
									if owner.Value == plr.Name then
										return tonumber(plot.Name:match("%d+"))
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
					return nil
				end

				local function GetInventory()
					local plotNumber = GetPlotNumber()

					if plotNumber then
						local plotItems = workspace:FindFirstChild("PlotItems")
						if plotItems then
							local plotFolder = plotItems:FindFirstChild("Plot" .. plotNumber)
							if plotFolder then
								return plotFolder
							end
						end
					end
					
					return workspace:FindFirstChild(plr.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
				end

				local originalCF = hrp.CFrame

				
				local tpConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
					if isToggled and hrp and targetPart and targetPart.Parent then
						hrp.CFrame = targetPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 5, 0)
					end
				end)

				local function createAndAttachKunai()
					if not isToggled then return end

					
					while plr:FindFirstChild("CanSpawnToy") and not plr.CanSpawnToy.Value do 
						if not isToggled then return end
						task.wait(0.1) 
					end

					task.spawn(function()
						SpawnToy:InvokeServer("NinjaKunai", hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 5, 15), Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
					end)

					task.wait(0.2)

					local inv = GetInventory()
					if not inv then return end

					local kunai
					for i = #inv:GetChildren(), 1, -1 do
						local obj = inv:GetChildren()[i]
						if obj.Name == "NinjaKunai" then
							kunai = obj
							break
						end
					end

					if not kunai then return end

					local stickyPart = kunai:WaitForChild("StickyPart", 1)
					local soundPart = kunai:WaitForChild("SoundPart", 1)

										if stickyPart and soundPart then
						pcall(function()
							SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(soundPart, soundPart.CFrame)
							SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(soundPart, torso.CFrame)
						end)
					end

										if stickyPart then
						local relativeCF = targetPart.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(targetPart.CFrame)
						pcall(function()
							StickyPartEvent:FireServer(stickyPart, targetPart, relativeCF)
						end)
					end

					task.wait(0.1)
				end

			
				for i = 1, 12 do
					if not isToggled then break end
					createAndAttachKunai()
				end

			
				if tpConnection then tpConnection:Disconnect() end
				task.wait(0.1)
				hrp.CFrame = originalCF
			end)
		end
	end
})

--[[
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
local NetOwnerRemote = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("GrabEvents"):WaitForChild("SetNetworkOwner")

local LockConfig = {
	TargetPlayer = nil,
	IsLockOn = false,
	SendInterval = 0.06 
}

--local activeLockCoroutine = nil

function getPlayerList()
	local list = {}
	for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		table.insert(list, p.DisplayName .. " (@" .. p.Name .. ")")
	end
	return list
end

local function getPlayerFromString(str)
	if not str then return nil end
	for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if str == p.DisplayName .. " (@" .. p.Name .. ")" then
			return p
		end
	end
	return nil
end

local function stopLock()
	LockConfig.IsLockOn = false
	if activeLockCoroutine then
		task.cancel(activeLockCoroutine)
		activeLockCoroutine = nil
	end
end

local function startLock()
	if not LockConfig.IsLockOn then return end
	if activeLockCoroutine then task.cancel(activeLockCoroutine) end

	activeLockCoroutine = task.spawn(function()
		while LockConfig.IsLockOn do
			local targetPlayer = getPlayerFromString(LockConfig.TargetPlayer)
			if targetPlayer and targetPlayer.Character then
				local targetHRP = targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if targetHRP then
					
					local targetCF = targetHRP.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 25, 0)

				
					for _, part in ipairs(targetPlayer.Character:GetDescendants()) do
						if part:IsA("BasePart") then
							pcall(function()
								NetOwnerRemote:FireServer(part, targetCF)
							end)
						end
					end
				end
			end
			task.wait(LockConfig.SendInterval)
		end
	end)
end

WTab:AddSection({
	Name = "네트워크 킥"
})

WTab:AddDropdown({
	Name = "타겟 고르기",
	Default = "",
	Options = getPlayerList(),
	Callback = function(Value)
		LockConfig.TargetPlayer = Value
	end    
})

WTab:AddButton({
	Name = "타겟 목록 새로고침",
	Callback = function()
		LockPlayerDropdown:Refresh(getPlayerList(), true)
	end    
})

WTab:AddToggle({
	Name = "Network kick (beta)",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(Value)
		LockConfig.IsLockOn = Value
		if Value then
			startLock()
		else
			stopLock()
		end
	end    
})
]]
end
end
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local Stats = game:GetService("Stats")
local RbxAnalyticsService = game:GetService("RbxAnalyticsService")

local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

local ownerIDs = {
    [5352731855] = true,
    [2932138780] = true,
    [11000044399] = true
}

local isOwner = ownerIDs[LocalPlayer.UserId] or false
local userRole = isOwner and "Owner" or "User"
local userRank = isOwner and "Owner Rank" or "Premium Rank"

local hwid = "Unknown"
pcall(function()
    hwid = RbxAnalyticsService:GetClientId()
end)


local ExecSection = UDTab:AddSection({
    Name = "user information"
})

local dispName = LocalPlayer.DisplayName
local realName = LocalPlayer.Name
ExecSection:AddLabel("name: " .. dispName .. " (@" .. realName .. ")")

ExecSection:AddLabel("HWID: " .. hwid)

--ExecSection:AddLabel("IP: 기밀")

ExecSection:AddLabel("User status: " .. userRole)

ExecSection:AddLabel("executor rating: " .. userRank)

local PingLabel = ExecSection:AddLabel("Ping: ...")
local FPSLabel = ExecSection:AddLabel("FPS: ...")

task.spawn(function()
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local lastTime = os.clock()
    local frameCount = 0
    
    RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        frameCount = frameCount + 1
        local currentTime = os.clock()
        if currentTime - lastTime >= 1 then
            local fps = math.floor(frameCount / (currentTime - lastTime))
            FPSLabel:Set("FPS: " .. tostring(fps))
            frameCount = 0
            lastTime = currentTime
        end
    end)
    
    while task.wait(1) do
        pcall(function()
            local ping = math.floor(Stats.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue())
            PingLabel:Set("ping: " .. tostring(ping) .. " ms")
        end)
    end
end)
end
local success = pcall(function()
    loadstring(game:HttpGet("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/qwerqwerg1u/RRTS/main/씨발년들"))()
end)

if not success then
    warn("[Junkie] failed to load the loader.(Load Failed)")
    return
end
